### [1] [Automated Standardization of Legacy Biomedical Metadata Using an Ontology-Constrained LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08552)
*Josef Hardi,Martin J. O'Connor,Marcos Martinez-Romero,Jean G. Rosario,Stephen A. Fisher,Mark A. Musen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LLM结合实时术语服务工具，提升生物医学元数据标准化准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学元数据常不完整且不符合社区标准，限制了数据集的可发现性、互操作性和重用性。现有报告指南缺乏机器可操作表示，而传统LLM方法仅将约束作为静态文本提示，依赖模型训练知识。

Method: 提出基于LLM的元数据标准化系统，实时查询权威生物医学术语服务，按需检索规范正确的词汇术语。在HuBMAP的839个遗留元数据记录上评估，使用专家策划的金标准进行精确匹配评估。

Result: 评估显示，通过实时工具访问增强LLM，在受本体约束和非本体约束的字段上，预测准确性均一致优于单独使用LLM。

Conclusion: 该方法为生物医学元数据自动化标准化提供了一种实用、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific metadata are often incomplete and noncompliant with community standards, limiting dataset findability, interoperability, and reuse. When reporting guidelines exist, they typically lack machine-actionable representations. Producing FAIR datasets requires encoding metadata standards as machine-actionable templates with rich field specifications and precise value constraints. Recent work has shown that LLMs guided by field names and ontology constraints can improve metadata standardization, but these approaches treat constraints as static text prompts, relying on the model's training knowledge alone. We present an LLM-based metadata standardization system that queries authoritative biomedical terminology services in real time to retrieve canonically correct vocabulary terms on demand. We evaluate this approach on 839 legacy metadata records from the Human BioMolecular Atlas Program (HuBMAP) using an expert-curated gold standard for exact-match assessment. Our evaluation shows that augmenting the LLM with real-time tool access consistently improves prediction accuracy over the LLM alone across both ontology-constrained and non-ontology-constrained fields, demonstrating a practical, scalable approach to automated standardization of biomedical metadata.

</details>


### [2] [QCFuse: Query-Centric Cache Fusion for Efficient RAG Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08585)
*Jianxin Yan,Zeheng Qian,Wangze Ni,Zhitao Shen,Zhiping Wang,Haoyang Li,Jia Zhu,Lei Chen,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: QCFuse：一种基于用户查询的KV缓存融合系统，通过语义摘要锚点增强查询表示，选择性重计算查询相关token，在保持精度的同时将LLM响应效率提升40%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓存融合方法主要依赖局部视角进行token选择，缺乏用户查询的全局感知。利用全局感知具有挑战性，因为获取上下文感知的查询表示成本高，且高效注意力分析需要严格的流水线约束

Method: QCFuse利用语义摘要锚点增强查询表示，选择性重计算查询相关token以提高准确性，基于最关键Transformer层的注意力分布更新token，同时保持流水线结构的高效性

Result: 在真实数据集上的评估表明，QCFuse在保持同等准确性的同时，将LLM的响应效率显著提升40%。在某些场景下，QCFuse还实现了注意力去噪效果，获得更高的响应准确性

Conclusion: QCFuse展示了在LLM推理优化中的巨大潜力，通过查询中心的KV缓存融合方法，在保持精度的同时显著提升效率，并在某些情况下还能提高准确性

Abstract: Cache fusion accelerates generation process of LLMs equipped with RAG through KV caching and selective token recomputation, thereby reducing computational costs and improving efficiency. However, existing methods primarily rely on local perspectives for token selection and lack global awareness from the user query. Utilizing this global awareness is challenging due to the high cost of obtaining context-aware query representations and the strict pipeline constraints required for efficient attention analysis. Thus, this demonstration introduces QCFuse, an innovative KV cache fusion system centered on the user query. QCFuse leverages semantic summary anchors to enhance query representations and selectively recomputes query-related tokens to improve accuracy, updating tokens based on the attention distribution of the most critical Transformer layer to preserve the high efficiency of the pipeline structure. Evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that QCFuse significantly improves the response efficiency of LLMs by 40\% while maintaining equivalent accuracy compared to current methods. Additionally, in certain scenarios, QCFuse achieves an attention denoising effect that yields higher response accuracy, demonstrating substantial potential in the optimization of LLM inference.

</details>


### [3] [Evaluating Data Quality Tools: Measurement Capabilities and LLM Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09163)
*Tobias Rehberger,Thomas Hütter,Lisa Ehrlinger,Wolfram Wöß*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 评估六大数据质量工具（Great Expectations、Deequ、Evidently、Informatica、Experian、Ataccama）在规则定义、重复检测、指标聚合和不确定性处理方面的能力，并分析其LLM集成现状


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据质量对可靠分析和运营效率至关重要，市场上涌现了从轻量级开源库到企业级平台的多种数据质量管理工具。本研究旨在系统评估主流数据质量工具的功能特性，特别关注它们对大型语言模型（LLM）的集成程度，为组织选择合适工具提供参考依据。

Method: 基于公司合作伙伴的实际用例制定评估标准，包括规则定义、重复检测、指标聚合和不确定性处理四个维度。对六款工具（Great Expectations、Deequ、Evidently、Informatica、Experian、Ataccama）进行系统性评估，并特别分析各工具对LLM的集成方式和程度。

Result: 专有工具（Informatica、Experian、Ataccama）提供更全面的测量功能和新兴的LLM辅助功能，而开源工具（Great Expectations、Deequ、Evidently）虽然灵活但需要更高的实施成本。所有工具中，LLM集成仅限于规则创建工作流，尚未有任何工具支持通过LLM直接进行数据验证。

Conclusion: 当前数据质量工具在功能完备性和易用性方面存在权衡：专有工具提供更全面的解决方案和LLM辅助，开源工具则提供更大的灵活性但实施成本更高。LLM在数据质量领域的应用仍处于早期阶段，主要局限于规则创建，直接的数据验证功能尚未实现，这为未来工具发展指明了方向。

Abstract: High data quality is critical for reliable analytics and operational efficiency. A growing ecosystem of tools has emerged to support data quality management, ranging from lightweight open-source libraries to comprehensive enterprise platforms. This paper evaluates six data quality tools: Great Expectations, Deequ, Evidently, Informatica, Experian, and Ataccama. The evaluation criteria cover rule definition, duplicate detection, metric aggregation, and uncertainty handling, and were derived from real-world use cases of company partners. We further examine to what extent these tools integrate Large Language Models (LLMs). Our findings show that proprietary tools offer more comprehensive measurement features and emerging LLM-based assistance, while open-source tools provide flexibility at the cost of higher implementation effort. Across all tools, LLM integration remains limited to rule creation workflows. Direct data validation through LLMs is not yet supported by any of the evaluated tools.

</details>


### [4] [Decoupling Vector Data and Index Storage for Space Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09173)
*Yuanming Ren,Juncheng Zhang,Yanjing Ren,Rui Yang,Di Wu,Patrick P. C. Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DecoupleVS是一个解耦的向量存储管理框架，通过分离向量数据和辅助索引元数据来优化磁盘ANN系统的存储效率，减少存储空间达58.7%，同时保持高性能和高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁盘ANN系统将向量数据和辅助索引元数据共置，导致存储开销大、查询时读取过多、更新时写入放大严重，需要更高效的存储管理方案。

Method: 提出DecoupleVS解耦框架，分离向量数据和辅助索引元数据，采用专门的压缩、数据布局、查询和更新技术进行优化。

Result: 在真实世界公开和专有的十亿级数据集上评估，DecoupleVS减少存储空间达58.7%，同时保持竞争性或改进的查询和更新性能。

Conclusion: DecoupleVS通过解耦向量存储管理，显著降低了磁盘ANN系统的存储开销，同时维持了高性能和高精度，为大规模向量数据集管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Managing large-scale vector datasets with disk-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) systems faces critical efficiency challenges stemming from the co-location of vector data and auxiliary index metadata. Our analysis of state-of-the-art ANNS systems reveals that such co-location incurs substantial storage overhead, generates excessive reads during search queries, and causes severe write amplification during updates. We present DecoupleVS, a decoupled vector storage management framework that enables specialized optimizations for vector data and auxiliary index metadata. DecoupleVS incorporates various design techniques for effective compression, data layouts, search queries, and updates, so as to significantly reduce storage space, while maintaining high search and update performance and high search accuracy. Evaluation on real-world public and proprietary billion-scale datasets shows that DecoupleVS reduces storage space by up to 58.7\%, while delivering competitive or improved search query and update performance, compared to state-of-the-art monolithic disk-based ANNS systems.

</details>


### [5] [A Catalog of Data Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09277)
*Divya Bhadauria,Hazar Harmouch,Felix Naumann,Divesh Srivastava,Lisa Ehrlinger*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个包含35种错误类型的综合分类法，涵盖数据错误和错误指标，分为缺失、不正确和冗余三类，为数据质量工具提供系统化的错误检测和清洗框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据库中数据错误普遍存在，严重影响下游应用。现有错误分类法存在局限性：1）多为非正式分类，未覆盖所有错误类型；2）未充分考虑AI时代需要处理的统计错误（如偏差、异常值）；3）术语使用不一致。需要更全面、正式的错误分类法来支持系统化的数据质量改进。

Method: 1）识别并定义35种不同的错误类型，包括数据错误（如缺失值、重复元组）和错误指标（如异常值、偏差）；2）将错误分为三个互斥类别：缺失、不正确和冗余；3）为每种错误类型提供正式定义和实际示例；4）统一相关工作中的术语不一致问题。

Result: 提出了一个全面的错误分类目录，包含：1）35种错误类型的正式定义；2）实际示例说明；3）三类分类框架（缺失、不正确、冗余）；4）术语标准化。该目录为研究人员和从业者提供了系统化的错误处理框架，支持针对特定错误类型实施检测和清洗策略。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了一个全面、正式的数据错误分类法，解决了现有分类法的局限性。通过涵盖传统数据错误和新兴统计错误指标，该目录能够支持更系统化的数据质量改进工作，为数据质量工具的开发和应用提供理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Data errors are widespread in real-world databases and severely impact downstream applications, such as machine learning pipelines or business analytics reports. Causes of such errors are manifold and can arise during both the design phase and the operational phase of a database. Some error types, such as missing values, duplicate tuples, or constraint violations, are widely recognized; others, such as disguised missing values or word transpositions, remain underexplored. Existing attempts to define and classify errors in data offer valuable but limited taxonomies, mostly informal and not covering the full range of error types. With the rise of AI, practitioners must increasingly detect and correct statistical errors such as bias and outliers, which are rarely considered within existing error taxonomies. This catalog presents a comprehensive list of 35 distinct error types, including both data errors (e.g., missing values, duplicate tuples) and error indicators (e.g., outliers, bias) for tabular data, classified into three non-overlapping categories: missing, incorrect, and redundant. For each error type, we provide a formal definition and practical example, and resolve terminological inconsistencies across related work. Our catalog enables researchers and practitioners to address various error types and systematically implement error-specific detection and cleaning strategies in data quality tools.

</details>

### [6] [A Domain-Theoretic Foundation for Imprecise Probability and Credal Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09272)
*Abbas Edalat,Pietro Di Gianantonio,Amin Farjudian*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个基于域理论的框架，用于在一般拓扑空间上进行不精确概率推理和推断，统一处理事件描述不完整和概率分布集合两种不确定性形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率推理框架通常假设事件描述完整且概率分布精确，但在实际应用中常面临事件描述不完整（部分信息）和概率分布不精确（分布集合）两种不确定性。需要建立一个统一的数学框架来处理这两种互补类型的不精确性，并支持稳健的概率推断。

Method: 1. 在具有可数基连续开集格的拓扑空间上构建域理论框架；2. 处理事件描述不完整和概率分布集合（信任集）两种不确定性；3. 建立条件概率理论，推导贝叶斯更新推理规则；4. 扩展为不精确概率事件的条件独立性理论；5. 为条件概率、贝叶斯更新和条件独立性构建逻辑谓词，获得完备性结果；6. 构建从任意信任集到区间域的Scott连续映射；7. 引入由具有不精确概率权重的迭代函数系统生成的新型信任集族。

Result: 1. 建立了统一处理两种不确定性形式的域理论框架；2. 推导了在存在不精确性时的贝叶斯更新推理规则；3. 获得了条件概率逻辑谓词的完备性结果；4. 构建了从信任集到区间域的Scott连续映射，实现了容量理论和Choquet积分的域理论表达；5. 引入了由迭代函数系统生成的新型信任集族，扩展了计算上可处理的不精确概率模型范围。

Conclusion: 该研究构建了一个可计算的框架，统一了逻辑、拓扑和测度论视角下的不确定性处理，支持在部分信息和集合值信息下的稳健概率推断。该框架为不精确概率推理提供了坚实的数学基础，并扩展了计算上可处理的不精确概率模型类型。

Abstract: We develop a domain-theoretic framework for imprecise probability reasoning and inference on general topological spaces with a countably based continuous lattice of open sets. We address two distinct forms of uncertainty: partial or incomplete event descriptions, and sets of probability distributions as represented by credal sets -- as well as their combination. Within this framework, we construct a theory of conditional probability and derive novel inference rules for performing Bayesian updating in the presence of these two complementary types of imprecision. These results are extended to a theory of conditional independence for imprecise probabilistic events. We also formulate logical predicates for conditional probability, Bayesian updating, and conditional independence, and we obtain the relevant soundness and completeness results. A key contribution is the construction of a Scott-continuous mapping from any credal set to the domain of intervals, providing a domain-theoretic realisation of classical results from capacity theory and Choquet integration. Finally, we introduce and study a new family of credal sets generated by iterated function systems with imprecise probability weights, broadening the scope of computationally tractable imprecise probabilistic models. The resulting computable framework unifies logical, topological, and measure-theoretic perspectives on uncertainty, supporting robust probabilistic inference under partial and set-valued information.

</details>

### [7] [Beyond the Individual: Virtualizing Multi-Disciplinary Reasoning for Clinical Intake via Collaborative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08927)
*Huangwei Chen,Wu Li,Junhao Jia,Yining Chen,Xiaotao Pang,Ya-Long Chen,Li Gonghui,Haishuai Wang,Jiajun Bu,Lei Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Aegle是一个同步虚拟多学科团队框架，通过基于图的多智能体架构将MDT级推理引入门诊咨询，在文档质量和咨询能力方面优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门诊初诊对临床决策至关重要，但通常由单个医生在时间压力下进行，容易产生认知偏差和证据收集不完整。虽然多学科团队（MDT）能降低这些风险，但成本高且难以实时扩展。

Method: 提出Aegle同步虚拟MDT框架，采用基于图的多智能体架构。使用结构化SOAP表示形式化咨询状态，将证据收集与诊断推理分离以提高可追溯性和偏差控制。编排器动态激活专科智能体，执行解耦的并行推理，然后由聚合器整合为连贯的临床记录。

Result: 在ClinicalBench和真实世界RAPID-IPN数据集（涵盖24个科室和53个指标）上的实验表明，Aegle在文档质量和咨询能力方面持续优于最先进的专有和开源模型，同时提高了最终诊断准确性。

Conclusion: Aegle通过虚拟MDT框架成功将多学科团队级推理引入门诊咨询，解决了传统单医生咨询的局限性，同时避免了实体MDT的成本和可扩展性问题。

Abstract: The initial outpatient consultation is critical for clinical decision-making, yet it is often conducted by a single physician under time pressure, making it prone to cognitive biases and incomplete evidence capture. Although the Multi-Disciplinary Team (MDT) reduces these risks, they are costly and difficult to scale to real-time intake. We propose Aegle, a synchronous virtual MDT framework that brings MDT-level reasoning to outpatient consultations via a graph-based multi-agent architecture. Aegle formalizes the consultation state using a structured SOAP representation, separating evidence collection from diagnostic reasoning to improve traceability and bias control. An orchestrator dynamically activates specialist agents, which perform decoupled parallel reasoning and are subsequently integrated by an aggregator into a coherent clinical note. Experiments on ClinicalBench and a real-world RAPID-IPN dataset across 24 departments and 53 metrics show that Aegle consistently outperforms state-of-the-art proprietary and open-source models in documentation quality and consultation capability, while also improving final diagnosis accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/HovChen/Aegle.

</details>


### [8] [Aligned Agents, Biased Swarm: Measuring Bias Amplification in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08963)
*Keyu Li,Jin Gao,Dequan Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究发现多智能体系统的基本拓扑结构和反馈循环会放大偏见，而非稀释偏见，即使个体智能体中立运行，系统架构的复杂性也会加剧偏见传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在复杂工作流中部署日益增多，但其涌现特性特别是偏见积累机制仍未被充分理解。由于现实世界MAS过于复杂难以全面分析，需要首先隔离其基础机制来评估伦理鲁棒性。

Method: 引入Discrim-Eval-Open开放基准测试，通过强制跨人口群体比较判断绕过个体模型中立性。分析不同结构中的偏见级联效应，研究基本MAS拓扑和反馈循环如何影响偏见传播。

Result: 架构复杂性经常加剧偏见而非缓解偏见，即使孤立智能体中立运行也会出现系统性放大效应。发现"触发脆弱性"现象，注入纯客观上下文会急剧加速极化。

Conclusion: 通过剥离高级群体复杂性来研究基础动态，建立关键基线：结构复杂性不能保证伦理鲁棒性。结构化工作流充当回音室，将微小随机偏见放大为系统性极化。

Abstract: While Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) are increasingly deployed for complex workflows, their emergent properties-particularly the accumulation of bias-remain poorly understood. Because real-world MAS are too complex to analyze entirely, evaluating their ethical robustness requires first isolating their foundational mechanics. In this work, we conduct a baseline empirical study investigating how basic MAS topologies and feedback loops influence prejudice. Contrary to the assumption that multi-agent collaboration naturally dilutes bias, we hypothesize that structured workflows act as echo chambers, amplifying minor stochastic biases into systemic polarization. To evaluate this, we introduce Discrim-Eval-Open, an open-ended benchmark that bypasses individual model neutrality through forced comparative judgments across demographic groups. Analyzing bias cascades across various structures reveals that architectural sophistication frequently exacerbates bias rather than mitigating it. We observe systemic amplification even when isolated agents operate neutrally, and identify a 'Trigger Vulnerability' where injecting purely objective context drastically accelerates polarization. By stripping away advanced swarm complexity to study foundational dynamics, we establish a crucial baseline: structural complexity does not guarantee ethical robustness. Our code is available at https://github.com/weizhihao1/MAS-Bias.

</details>


### [9] [Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning for Low-Carbon P2P Energy Trading among Self-Interested Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08973)
*Junhao Ren,Honglin Gao,Lan Zhao,Qiyu Kang,Gaoxi Xiao,Yajuan Sun*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出多智能体强化学习框架，用于微电网参与P2P电力交易的日前调度，通过储能套利优化自身利润，提高可再生能源利用率并减少高碳电力依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源发电和需求动态的不确定性给日前调度带来挑战，需要提高可再生能源渗透率并维持日内平衡，同时促进微电网参与P2P电力交易。

Method: 开发多智能体强化学习框架，每个微电网独立投标价格和数量，通过储能套利在时变主网电价下优化自身利润，并提出协调交易和促进激励相容的市场出清机制。

Result: 仿真结果表明，学习到的投标策略提高了可再生能源利用率，减少了对高碳电力的依赖，同时增加了社区层面的经济福利，实现了减排和本地繁荣的双赢局面。

Conclusion: 该多智能体强化学习框架为自利微电网参与P2P电力交易提供了有效解决方案，能够同时实现环境效益和经济效益，促进可再生能源的高效利用和社区可持续发展。

Abstract: Uncertainties in renewable generation and demand dynamics challenge day-ahead scheduling. To enhance renewable penetration and maintain intra-day balance, we develop a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for self-interested microgrids participating in peer-to-peer (P2P) electricity trading. Each microgrid independently bids both price and quantity while optimizing its own profit via storage arbitrage under time-varying main-grid prices. A market-clearing mechanism coordinating trades and promoting incentive compatibility is proposed. Simulation results show that the learned bidding policy improves renewable utilization and reduces reliance on high-carbon electricity, while increasing community-level economic welfare, delivering a win-win situation in emission reduction and local prosperity.

</details>


### [10] [Plasticity-Enhanced Multi-Agent Mixture of Experts for Dynamic Objective Adaptation in UAVs-Assisted Emergency Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09028)
*Wen Qiu,Zhiqiang He,Wei Zhao,Hiroshi Masui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: PE-MAMoE框架通过混合专家架构和相位控制器增强无人机集群在动态环境中的可塑性，解决深度强化学习策略在非平稳场景下的表示崩溃和神经元休眠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机作为空中基站在灾后快速恢复通信时，用户移动性和流量需求的突变导致服务质量权衡变化并引发强非平稳性，传统深度强化学习策略在此类场景下会出现可塑性损失，表现为表示崩溃和神经元休眠，从而损害适应能力。

Method: 提出PE-MAMoE框架，基于多智能体近端策略优化构建集中训练分散执行架构。每个无人机配备稀疏门控混合专家actor，路由器每步选择一个专家。非参数相位控制器在相位切换后注入短暂的专家专用随机扰动，重置动作对数标准差，退火熵和学习率，调度路由器温度，以重新可塑性化策略而不破坏安全行为。

Result: 在移动用户和3GPP风格信道的相位驱动模拟器中，PE-MAMoE相比最佳基线将归一化四分位均值回报提高了26.3%，服务用户容量增加了12.8%，碰撞减少了约75%。诊断确认了持续更高的专家特征秩和在机制切换时的周期性休眠神经元恢复。

Conclusion: PE-MAMoE通过混合专家架构和相位控制机制有效解决了深度强化学习在非平稳环境中的可塑性损失问题，显著提升了无人机集群在动态通信场景中的适应性和性能，理论分析表明跟踪误差与环境变化和累积噪声能量相关。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles serving as aerial base stations can rapidly restore connectivity after disasters, yet abrupt changes in user mobility and traffic demands shift the quality of service trade-offs and induce strong non-stationarity. Deep reinforcement learning policies suffer from plasticity loss under such shifts, as representation collapse and neuron dormancy impair adaptation. We propose plasticity enhanced multi-agent mixture of experts (PE-MAMoE), a centralized training with decentralized execution framework built on multi-agent proximal policy optimization. PE-MAMoE equips each UAV with a sparsely gated mixture of experts actor whose router selects a single specialist per step. A non-parametric Phase Controller injects brief, expert-only stochastic perturbations after phase switches, resets the action log-standard-deviation, anneals entropy and learning rate, and schedules the router temperature, all to re-plasticize the policy without destabilizing safe behaviors. We derive a dynamic regret bound showing the tracking error scales with both environment variation and cumulative noise energy. In a phase-driven simulator with mobile users and 3GPP-style channels, PE-MAMoE improves normalized interquartile mean return by 26.3\% over the best baseline, increases served-user capacity by 12.8\%, and reduces collisions by approximately 75\%. Diagnostics confirm persistently higher expert feature rank and periodic dormant-neuron recovery at regime switches.

</details>


### [11] [Risk-seeking conservative policy iteration with agent-state based policies for Dec-POMDPs with guaranteed convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09495)
*Amit Sinha,Matthieu Geist,Aditya Mahajan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在有限内存约束下求解Dec-POMDP问题的迭代最优响应算法，通过风险寻求目标函数和改进的策略迭代，在多项式时间内获得局部最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dec-POMDP的最优求解是NEXP完全的，需要基于完整历史记录的策略。然而实际应用中由于计算能力限制，需要更紧凑的策略（有限内存状态）。虽然这类基于智能体状态的策略类可能不包含最优解，但在该类中找到最佳策略仍具有实际意义。

Method: 采用迭代最优响应风格的算法，保证单调改进并在多项式时间内收敛到局部最优。使用改进的目标函数（激励风险寻求）和保守的策略迭代更新，以获得更好的局部最优解。

Result: 在多个基准Dec-POMDP上的实验结果表明，该方法性能与最先进方法相当，在有限内存下实现接近最优的性能，同时保持多项式运行时间。增加智能体状态（更大内存）能带来更好的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为Dec-POMDP问题中引入智能体内存约束提供了一种新颖的方式，在有限内存条件下实现了良好的性能与计算效率的平衡。

Abstract: Optimally solving decentralized decision-making problems modeled as Dec-POMDPs is known to be NEXP-complete. These optimal solutions are policies based on the entire history of observations and actions of an agent. However, some applications may require more compact policies because of limited compute capabilities, which can be modeled by considering a limited number of memory states (or agent states). While such an agent-state based policy class may not contain the optimal solution, it is still of practical interest to find the best agent-state policy within the class. We focus on an iterated best response style algorithm which guarantees monotonic improvements and convergence to a local optimum in polynomial runtime in the Dec-POMDP model size. In order to obtain a better local optimum, we use a modified objective which incentivizes risk-seeking alongside a conservative policy iteration update. Our empirical results show that our approach performs as well as state-of-the-art approaches on several benchmark Dec-POMDPs, achieving near-optimal performance while having polynomial runtime despite the limited memory. We also show that using more agent states (a larger memory) leads to greater performance. Our approach provides a novel way of incorporating memory constraints on the agents in the Dec-POMDP problem.

</details>

### [12] [LEGO: Latent-space Exploration for Geometry-aware Optimization of Humanoid Kinematic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08636)
*Jihwan Yoon,Taemoon Jeong,Jeongeun Park,Chanwoo Kim,Jaewoon Kwon,Yonghyeon Lee,Kyungjae Lee,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的机器人设计新范式，通过从现有机械设计学习设计搜索空间，并利用人类运动数据定义损失函数，实现人形机器人上半身设计的自动化优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人形态和运动学设计依赖人类直觉，缺乏系统化基础。运动设计协同优化面临两大挑战：巨大的非结构化设计空间和任务特定损失函数构建困难。

Method: 采用基于螺旋理论的关节轴表示和等距流形学习，构建紧凑、几何保持的人形机器人上半身设计潜在空间；使用运动重定向和Procrustes分析从人类运动数据直接定义损失函数；在潜在空间中使用无梯度优化进行设计优化。

Result: 建立了一个数据驱动机器人设计的原理性框架，证明利用现有设计和人类运动可以有效指导新型机器人设计的自动化发现。

Conclusion: 提出的新范式通过最小化人类参与，从现有机械设计学习设计搜索空间，并从人类运动数据直接定义损失，为机器人设计的自动化提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: Designing robot morphologies and kinematics has traditionally relied on human intuition, with little systematic foundation. Motion-design co-optimization offers a promising path toward automation, but two major challenges remain: (i) the vast, unstructured design space and (ii) the difficulty of constructing task-specific loss functions. We propose a new paradigm that minimizes human involvement by (i) learning the design search space from existing mechanical designs, rather than hand-crafting it, and (ii) defining the loss directly from human motion data via motion retargeting and Procrustes analysis. Using screw-theory-based joint axis representation and isometric manifold learning, we construct a compact, geometry-preserving latent space of humanoid upper body designs in which optimization is tractable. We then solve design optimization in this latent space using gradient-free optimization. Our approach establishes a principled framework for data-driven robot design and demonstrates that leveraging existing designs and human motion can effectively guide the automated discovery of novel robot design.

</details>


### [13] [Generative Simulation for Policy Learning in Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08664)
*Junxiang Wang,Xinwen Xu,Tiancheng Wu,Julian Millan,Nir Pechuk,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个用于物理人机交互的零样本"文本到仿真到现实"生成式仿真框架，通过自然语言提示自动合成多样化pHRI场景，实现大规模合成数据收集和视觉模仿学习策略训练


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发自主物理人机交互系统受限于大规模训练数据的稀缺性，难以学习适用于真实世界应用的鲁棒机器人行为

Method: 利用大语言模型和视觉语言模型，通过自然语言提示程序化生成软体人体模型、场景布局和机器人运动轨迹，收集大规模合成演示数据集，训练基于分割点云的视觉模仿学习策略

Result: 在抓挠和洗澡两个辅助任务上，学习到的策略成功实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，成功率超过80%，并对非脚本化人体运动表现出鲁棒性

Conclusion: 提出了首个用于pHRI应用的生成式仿真流水线，实现了仿真环境合成、数据收集和策略学习的自动化，为物理人机交互系统开发提供了新范式

Abstract: Developing autonomous physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) systems is limited by the scarcity of large-scale training data to learn robust robot behaviors for real-world applications. In this paper, we introduce a zero-shot "text2sim2real" generative simulation framework that automatically synthesizes diverse pHRI scenarios from high-level natural-language prompts. Leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), our pipeline procedurally generates soft-body human models, scene layouts, and robot motion trajectories for assistive tasks. We utilize this framework to autonomously collect large-scale synthetic demonstration datasets and then train vision-based imitation learning policies operating on segmented point clouds. We evaluate our approach through a user study on two physically assistive tasks: scratching and bathing. Our learned policies successfully achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, attaining success rates exceeding 80% and demonstrating resilience to unscripted human motion. Overall, we introduce the first generative simulation pipeline for pHRI applications, automating simulation environment synthesis, data collection, and policy learning. Additional information may be found on our project website: https://rchi-lab.github.io/gen_phri/

</details>


### [14] [Online Intention Prediction via Control-Informed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09303)
*Tianyu Zhou,Zihao Liang,Zehui Lu,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在线意图预测框架，用于实时估计自主系统的目标状态，即使意图随时间变化且系统动态或目标包含未知参数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在复杂环境中需要实时预测时变意图，但现有方法难以处理系统动态或目标函数中的未知参数，且需要适应意图随时间变化的情况

Method: 将问题构建为逆最优控制/逆强化学习任务，将意图视为目标函数中的参数；采用移动时域策略折扣过时信息，结合在线控制知情学习实现高效梯度计算和在线参数更新

Result: 在不同噪声水平下的仿真和四旋翼无人机硬件实验中，该方法在复杂环境中实现了准确、自适应的意图预测

Conclusion: 提出的在线意图预测框架能够有效处理时变意图和未知参数，在真实自主系统中实现鲁棒的实时意图估计

Abstract: This paper presents an online intention prediction framework for estimating the goal state of autonomous systems in real time, even when intention is time-varying, and system dynamics or objectives include unknown parameters. The problem is formulated as an inverse optimal control / inverse reinforcement learning task, with the intention treated as a parameter in the objective. A shifting horizon strategy discounts outdated information, while online control-informed learning enables efficient gradient computation and online parameter updates. Simulations under varying noise levels and hardware experiments on a quadrotor drone demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves accurate, adaptive intention prediction in complex environments.

</details>


### [15] [Task-Aware Bimanual Affordance Prediction via VLM-Guided Semantic-Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08726)
*Fabian Hahne,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个分层框架，通过视觉语言模型进行任务感知的双手机器人操作预测，联合解决可操作区域定位和手臂分配问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手机器人操作需要同时考虑与物体的交互位置和手臂分配，这需要语义理解任务意图。现有方法要么将可操作性预测视为粗略的部分分割，要么依赖几何启发式进行手臂分配，无法联合推理任务相关的接触区域和手臂分配。

Method: 提出分层框架：1) 融合多视角RGB-D观测到一致的3D场景表示；2) 生成全局6自由度抓取候选；3) 查询视觉语言模型进行空间和语义过滤，获取每个物体上任务相关的可操作性区域以及手臂分配，确保几何有效性同时尊重任务语义。

Result: 在双手机器人平台上评估了9个真实世界操作任务，涵盖四类：并行操作、协调稳定、工具使用和人类交接。相比几何和语义基线，在任务导向抓取方面获得了持续更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 对可操作性和手臂分配的显式语义推理有助于在非结构化环境中实现可靠的双手机器人操作，视觉语言模型能够泛化到不同物体类别和任务描述，无需类别特定训练。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation requires reasoning about where to interact with an object and which arm should perform each action, a joint affordance localization and arm allocation problem that geometry-only planners cannot resolve without semantic understanding of task intent. Existing approaches either treat affordance prediction as coarse part segmentation or rely on geometric heuristics for arm assignment, failing to jointly reason about task-relevant contact regions and arm allocation. We reframe bimanual manipulation as a joint affordance localization and arm allocation problem and propose a hierarchical framework for task-aware bimanual affordance prediction that leverages a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to generalize across object categories and task descriptions without requiring category-specific training. Our approach fuses multi-view RGB-D observations into a consistent 3D scene representation and generates global 6-DoF grasp candidates, which are then spatially and semantically filtered by querying the VLM for task-relevant affordance regions on each object, as well as for arm allocation to the individual objects, thereby ensuring geometric validity while respecting task semantics. We evaluate our method on a dual-arm platform across nine real-world manipulation tasks spanning four categories: parallel manipulation, coordinated stabilization, tool use, and human handover. Our approach achieves consistently higher task success rates than geometric and semantic baselines for task-oriented grasping, demonstrating that explicit semantic reasoning over affordances and arm allocation helps enable reliable bimanual manipulation in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [16] [Toward Hardware-Agnostic Quadrupedal World Models via Morphology Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08780)
*Mohamad H. Danesh,Chenhao Li,Amin Abyaneh,Anas Houssaini,Kirsty Ellis,Glen Berseth,Marco Hutter,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可泛化的四足机器人世界模型(QWM)，通过显式编码机器人形态学特征，实现跨不同四足机器人形态的零样本控制，解决了现有世界模型对特定硬件过拟合的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型存在硬件锁定的问题：针对特定机器人（如波士顿动力Spot）训练的模型无法泛化到其他机器人（如宇树Go1），因为模型过拟合于特定硬件约束而非学习通用的运动动力学。机器人形态的微小变化（如执行器动力学或肢体长度）就需要重新训练模型，这限制了世界模型的实用性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出Quadrupedal World Model (QWM)框架，通过显式地将机器人工程规格（物理形态）作为条件输入生成动力学模型，而不是隐式地从运动历史推断静态物理属性。方法包含物理形态编码器和奖励归一器，使模型能够作为神经模拟器在不同形态间泛化。

Result: 首次实现了能够在四足机器人形态间进行零样本泛化的世界模型。QWM作为四足机器人形态家族内的分布有界插值器，能够实现跨不同形态的零样本控制，显著提升了世界模型的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 这项工作代表了向形态条件化世界模型迈出的重要一步，虽然QWM是四足机器人形态家族内的插值器而非通用物理引擎，但它为腿部运动的形态条件化世界模型提供了有前景的框架，解决了硬件锁定问题并提升了零样本安全性和效率。

Abstract: World models promise a paradigm shift in robotics, where an agent learns the underlying physics of its environment once to enable efficient planning and behavior learning. However, current world models are often hardware-locked specialists: a model trained on a Boston Dynamics Spot robot fails catastrophically on a Unitree Go1 due to the mismatch in kinematic and dynamic properties, as the model overfits to specific embodiment constraints rather than capturing the universal locomotion dynamics. Consequently, a slight change in actuator dynamics or limb length necessitates training a new model from scratch. In this work, we take a step towards a framework for training a generalizable Quadrupedal World Model (QWM) that disentangles environmental dynamics from robot morphology. We address the limitations of implicit system identification, where treating static physical properties (like mass or limb length) as latent variables to be inferred from motion history creates an adaptation lag that can compromise zero-shot safety and efficiency. Instead, we explicitly condition the generative dynamics on the robot's engineering specifications. By integrating a physical morphology encoder and a reward normalizer, we enable the model to serve as a neural simulator capable of generalizing across morphologies. This capability unlocks zero-shot control across a range of embodiments. We introduce, for the first time, a world model that enables zero-shot generalization to new morphologies for locomotion. While we carefully study the limitations of our method, QWM operates as a distribution-bounded interpolator within the quadrupedal morphology family rather than a universal physics engine, this work represents a significant step toward morphology-conditioned world models for legged locomotion.

</details>


### [17] [One Interface, Many Robots: Unified Real-Time Low-Level Motion Planning for Collaborative Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08787)
*Yue Feng,Weicheng Huang,I-Ming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于协作机械臂实时低级运动规划的通用接口，通过多项式插值和二次规划求解器生成平滑轨迹，在离线绘图、动态抓取和遥操作三种场景中验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为异构硬件平台上的协作机械臂提供通用、可移植的实时低级运动规划接口，解决现有系统在硬件兼容性和实时控制方面的限制

Method: 基于WinGs Operating Studio中间件，提供最小化但灵活的末端执行器轨迹控制接口，采用n次多项式插值器和二次规划求解器生成具有精确位置、速度和加速度轮廓的平滑连续可微轨迹

Result: 在三种场景中成功验证：1)离线演示中机械臂准确绘制几何图形；2)可中断的低频重规划场景中抓取移动机器人上的动态物体；3)遥操作实验中一个机械臂控制另一个完成灵巧操作

Conclusion: 提出的方法提供了可靠、通用且易用的实时运动规划接口，能够有效支持协作机械臂在异构平台上的多样化应用

Abstract: This paper proposes a common interface for real-time low-level motion planning of collaborative robotic arms, aimed at enabling broader applicability and improved portability across heterogeneous hardware platforms. In previous work, we introduced WinGs Operating Studio (WOS), a middleware solution that abstracts diverse robotic components into uniform software resources and provides a broad suite of language-agnostic APIs. This paper specifically focuses on its minimal yet flexible interface for real-time end-effector trajectory control. By employing an n-degree polynomial interpolator in conjunction with a quadratic programming solver, the proposed method generates smooth, continuously differentiable trajectories with precise position, velocity, and acceleration profiles. We validate our approach in three distinct scenarios. First, in an offline demonstration, a collaborative arm accurately draws various geometric shapes on paper. Second, in an interruptible, low-frequency re-planning setting, a robotic manipulator grasps a dynamic object placed on a moving mobile robot. Finally, we conducted a teleoperation experiment in which one robotic arm controlled another to perform a series of dexterous manipulations, confirming the proposed method's reliability, versatility, and ease of use.

</details>


### [18] [Simulation of Adaptive Running with Flexible Sports Prosthesis using Reinforcement Learning of Hybrid-link System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08882)
*Yuta Shimane,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的单侧胫骨截肢者跑步运动仿真方法，使用混合连杆系统模拟叶片弹簧型运动假肢的柔性变形，分析不同虚拟假肢刚度对跑步性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动假肢的设计和选择通常依赖试错法，缺乏考虑人体运动与假肢变形相互作用的全面全身动力学分析。需要一种能够提供用户特定设计和选择有价值见解的仿真方法。

Method: 采用混合连杆系统，结合分段恒定应变模型表示假肢的柔性变形。基于强化学习的方法，结合运动捕捉数据的模仿学习和精确的假肢动力学计算，生成单侧胫骨截肢者的全身动态运动。

Result: 在不同虚拟假肢刚度条件下模拟跑步运动，分析仿真获得的运输代谢成本，发现刚度变化会影响跑步性能。证明了该方法在虚拟条件下进行仿真和分析的潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法能够模拟和分析与实际情况不同的虚拟条件下的运动，为运动假肢的用户特定设计和选择提供了有价值的仿真工具。

Abstract: This study proposes a reinforcement learning-based adaptive running motion simulation for a unilateral transtibial amputee with the flexibility of a leaf-spring-type sports prosthesis using hybrid-link system. The design and selection of sports prostheses often rely on trial and error. A comprehensive whole-body dynamics analysis that considers the interaction between human motion and prosthetic deformation could provide valuable insights for user-specific design and selection. The hybrid-link system facilitates whole-body dynamics analysis by incorporating the Piece-wise Constant Strain model to represent the flexible deformation of the prosthesis. Based on this system, the simulation methodology generates whole-body dynamic motions of a unilateral transtibial amputee through a reinforcement learning-based approach, which combines imitation learning from motion capture data with accurate prosthetic dynamics computation. We simulated running motions under different virtual prosthetic stiffness conditions and analyzed the metabolic cost of transport obtained from the simulations, suggesting that variations in stiffness influence running performance. Our findings demonstrate the potential of this approach for simulation and analysis under virtual conditions that differ from real conditions.

</details>


### [19] [HTNav: A Hybrid Navigation Framework with Tiered Structure for Urban Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08883)
*Chengjie Fan,Cong Pan,Zijian Liu,Ningzhong Liu,Jie Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HTNav：一种结合模仿学习与强化学习的混合导航框架，用于解决复杂城市环境中空中视觉语言导航的泛化性、长距离路径规划和空间连续性理解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中视觉语言导航方法在复杂城市环境中面临三大挑战：1）对未见场景的泛化能力不足；2）长距离路径规划性能欠佳；3）对空间连续性的理解不够充分。这些限制了其在物流配送、城市巡检等实际应用中的价值。

Method: 提出HTNav混合导航框架：1）集成模仿学习（IL）和强化学习（RL）的混合IL-RL框架；2）采用分阶段训练机制确保基础导航策略稳定性并增强环境探索能力；3）引入分层决策机制实现宏观路径规划与细粒度动作控制的协同交互；4）设计地图表示学习模块加深对开放领域空间连续性的理解。

Result: 在CityNav基准测试中，该方法在所有场景级别和任务难度上都达到了最先进的性能。实验结果表明，该框架显著提高了复杂城市环境中的导航精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HTNav框架通过混合IL-RL方法、分层决策机制和地图表示学习，有效解决了空中视觉语言导航在复杂城市环境中的关键挑战，为实际应用提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Inspired by the general Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) task, aerial VLN has attracted widespread attention, owing to its significant practical value in applications such as logistics delivery and urban inspection. However, existing methods face several challenges in complex urban environments, including insufficient generalization to unseen scenes, suboptimal performance in long-range path planning, and inadequate understanding of spatial continuity. To address these challenges, we propose HTNav, a new collaborative navigation framework that integrates Imitation Learning (IL) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) within a hybrid IL-RL framework. This framework adopts a staged training mechanism to ensure the stability of the basic navigation strategy while enhancing its environmental exploration capability. By integrating a tiered decision-making mechanism, it achieves collaborative interaction between macro-level path planning and fine-grained action control. Furthermore, a map representation learning module is introduced to deepen its understanding of spatial continuity in open domains. On the CityNav benchmark, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across all scene levels and task difficulties. Experimental results demonstrate that this framework significantly improves navigation precision and robustness in complex urban environments.

</details>


### [20] [AssemLM: Spatial Reasoning Multimodal Large Language Models for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08983)
*Zhi Jing,Jinbin Qiao,Ouyang Lu,Jicong Ao,Shuang Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AssemLM：面向机器人装配任务的空间多模态大语言模型，通过整合装配手册、点云和文本指令，实现6D装配姿态预测，填补了现有VLM在3D几何推理方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型主要依赖粗粒度的2D感知，缺乏对3D几何的精确推理能力，这在需要精细操作的机器人装配任务中尤为关键。现有模型无法处理装配任务所需的空间几何理解，特别是6D姿态预测。

Method: 提出AssemLM模型，采用专门的点云编码器捕获细粒度几何和旋转特征，将其集成到多模态语言模型中。同时构建AssemBench数据集，包含超过90万条带精确6D姿态标注的多模态样本，用于装配导向的空间推理评估。

Result: AssemLM在多种装配场景下实现了6D姿态推理的最先进性能。真实机器人评估表明，该模型能够支持现实环境中的细粒度多步骤装配执行，验证了其在机器人装配应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: AssemLM通过整合3D几何感知与高级推理，显著提升了机器人装配任务的空间推理能力。AssemBench填补了现有具身AI基准在3D几何推理评估方面的空白，为装配导向的空间推理研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, especially for fine-grained manipulation tasks such as robotic assembly. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit preliminary spatial awareness, they largely rely on coarse 2D perception and lack the ability to perform accurate reasoning over 3D geometry, which is crucial for precise assembly operations. To address this limitation, we propose AssemLM, a spatial multimodal large language model tailored for robotic assembly. AssemLM integrates assembly manuals, point clouds, and textual instructions to reason about and predict task-critical 6D assembly poses, enabling explicit geometric understanding throughout the assembly process. To effectively bridge raw 3D perception and high-level reasoning, we adopt a specialized point cloud encoder to capture fine-grained geometric and rotational features, which are then integrated into the multimodal language model to support accurate 3D spatial reasoning for assembly tasks. In addition, we construct AssemBench, a large-scale dataset and benchmark for assembly-oriented spatial reasoning, comprising over 900K multimodal samples with precise 6D pose annotations. AssemBench extends spatial reasoning evaluation beyond 2D and grounding tasks into full 3D geometric inference, filling a critical gap in existing embodied AI benchmarks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AssemLM achieves state-of-the-art performance in 6D pose reasoning across diverse assembly scenarios. Furthermore, real-robot evaluations show that our model can support fine-grained and multi-step assembly execution in real-world settings, demonstrating its potential for robotic assembly applications.

</details>


### [21] [V-CAGE: Vision-Closed-Loop Agentic Generation Engine for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09036)
*Yaru Liu,Ao-bo Wang,Nanyang Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V-CAGE是一个用于自主机器人数据合成的智能体框架，通过上下文感知的场景生成、闭环验证和感知驱动压缩，解决现有方法缺乏语义一致性和物理可行性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型需要大规模数据集，但传统场景生成方法缺乏上下文感知能力，导致合成环境语义信息不足且目标位置不可达，造成任务过早失败。

Method: 1) 引入修复引导的场景构建系统安排上下文感知布局；2) 集成功能元数据与基于视觉语言模型的闭环验证机制；3) 实现感知驱动压缩算法减少90%以上文件大小。

Result: V-CAGE实现了端到端自动化管道，能够高度可扩展地合成多样化、高质量的机器人操作数据集，同时保持下游VLA训练效果。

Conclusion: V-CAGE通过集中语义布局规划和视觉自验证，解决了大规模机器人数据集合成的关键挑战，为可扩展的VLA模型训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Scaling Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models requires massive datasets that are both semantically coherent and physically feasible. However, existing scene generation methods often lack context-awareness, making it difficult to synthesize high-fidelity environments embedded with rich semantic information, frequently resulting in unreachable target positions that cause tasks to fail prematurely. We present V-CAGE (Vision-Closed-loop Agentic Generation Engine), an agentic framework for autonomous robotic data synthesis. Unlike traditional scripted pipelines, V-CAGE operates as an embodied agentic system, leveraging foundation models to bridge high-level semantic reasoning with low-level physical interaction. Specifically, we introduce Inpainting-Guided Scene Construction to systematically arrange context-aware layouts, ensuring that the generated scenes are both semantically structured and kinematically reachable. To ensure trajectory correctness, we integrate functional metadata with a Vision-Language Model based closed-loop verification mechanism, acting as a visual critic to rigorously filter out silent failures and sever the error propagation chain. Finally, to overcome the storage bottleneck of massive video datasets, we implement a perceptually-driven compression algorithm that achieves over 90\% filesize reduction without compromising downstream VLA training efficacy. By centralizing semantic layout planning and visual self-verification, V-CAGE automates the end-to-end pipeline, enabling the highly scalable synthesis of diverse, high-quality robotic manipulation datasets.

</details>


### [22] [Towards Lifelong Aerial Autonomy: Geometric Memory Management for Continual Visual Place Recognition in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09038)
*Xingyu Shao,Zhiqiang Yan,Liangzheng Sun,Mengfan He,Chao Chen,Jinhui Zhang,Chunyu Li,Ziyang Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于空中视觉地点识别的异构记忆框架，通过解耦地理知识为静态卫星锚点和动态经验回放缓冲区，解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中视觉地点识别在变化环境条件下的鲁棒性对长期空中自主性至关重要。现有持续学习方法在处理地理特征严重类内变化时效果不佳，且面临严格的机载存储限制。

Method: 将空中VPR建模为基于任务的域增量学习问题，提出"学习与处置"管道，将地理知识解耦为静态卫星锚点（保留全局几何先验）和动态经验回放缓冲区（保留域特定特征）。引入基于空间约束的分配策略，根据样本难度或特征空间多样性优化缓冲区选择。

Result: 在21个多样化任务序列的基准测试中，多样性驱动的缓冲区选择比随机基线在知识保留方面提升7.8%。最大化结构多样性在可塑性与稳定性之间取得更好平衡，确保跨随机化序列的顺序无关鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对于终身空中自主系统中的灾难性遗忘问题，维持结构特征覆盖比样本难度更为关键。异构记忆框架通过解耦地理知识显著提升了空间泛化能力。

Abstract: Robust geo-localization in changing environmental conditions is critical for long-term aerial autonomy. While visual place recognition (VPR) models perform well when airborne views match the training domain, adapting them to shifting distributions during sequential missions triggers catastrophic forgetting. Existing continual learning (CL) methods often fail here because geographic features exhibit severe intra-class variations. In this work, we formulate aerial VPR as a mission-based domain-incremental learning (DIL) problem and propose a novel heterogeneous memory framework. To respect strict onboard storage constraints, our "Learn-and-Dispose" pipeline decouples geographic knowledge into static satellite anchors (preserving global geometric priors) and a dynamic experience replay buffer (retaining domain-specific features). We introduce a spatially-constrained allocation strategy that optimizes buffer selection based on sample difficulty or feature space diversity. To facilitate systematic assessment, we provide three evaluation criteria and a comprehensive benchmark derived from 21 diverse mission sequences. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our architecture significantly boosts spatial generalization; our diversity-driven buffer selection outperforms the random baseline by 7.8% in knowledge retention. Unlike class-mean preservation methods that fail in unstructured environments, maximizing structural diversity achieves a superior plasticity-stability balance and ensures order-agnostic robustness across randomized sequences. These results prove that maintaining structural feature coverage is more critical than sample difficulty for resolving catastrophic forgetting in lifelong aerial autonomy.

</details>


### [23] [On the Terminology and Geometric Aspects of Redundant Parallel Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09156)
*Andreas Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文为并联机器人提出统一的冗余分类术语，强调构型空间在区分运动学冗余和驱动冗余中的核心作用，并引入驱动度概念对并联机器人进行系统分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并联机器人存在运动学冗余和驱动冗余两种不同类型，但文献中对驱动冗余的分类不一致。需要建立统一的术语体系来清晰区分这两种冗余类型，并阐明构型空间几何在理解并联机器人奇异性和冗余驱动方案中的重要性。

Method: 引入以构型空间为核心的动力学模型，区分构型空间奇异、输出奇异和输入奇异。提出非线性控制系统，其动力学在构型空间上演化。引入驱动度概念作为独立控制向量场的数量，将并联机器人分类为全驱动和欠驱动系统。通过比较驱动度与自由度来分类驱动冗余。

Result: 建立了并联机器人冗余分类的统一术语体系。阐明了构型空间几何在理解奇异性和冗余驱动中的关键作用。证明了通过冗余驱动方案可以避免输入奇异。提出了基于驱动度与自由度关系的驱动冗余分类方法。

Conclusion: 构型空间是理解并联机器人冗余特性的核心概念。运动学冗余和驱动冗余需要明确区分，驱动度概念为并联机器人的驱动方案分类提供了系统框架。冗余驱动可以有效避免输入奇异，提高机器人的运动性能。

Abstract: Parallel kinematics machines (PKM) can exhibit kinematic as well as actuation redundancy. While the meaning of kinematic redundancy has been clarified already for serial manipulators, actuation redundancy, that is only possible in PKM, is differently classified in the literature. In this paper a consistent terminology for general redundant PKM is proposed. A kinematic model is introduced with the configuration space (c-space) as central part. The notion of kinematic redundancy is recalled for PKM. C-space, output, and input singularities are distinguished. The significance of the c-space geometry is emphasized, and it is pointed out geometrically that input singularities can be avoided by redundant actuation schemes. In order to distinguish different actuation schemes of PKM a non-linear control system is introduced whose dynamics evolves on the c-space. The degree of actuation (DOA) is introduced as the number of independent control vector fields, and PKM are classified as full-actuated and underactuated. Relating this DOA to the degree of freedom (DOF) allows to classify the actuation redundancy.

</details>


### [24] [Soft Electroadhesive Feet for Micro Aerial Robots Perching on Smooth and Curved Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09270)
*Chen Liu,Sonu Feroz,Ketao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了用于微型飞行机器人栖息的柔软可拉伸电粘附垫，通过实验比较了法向和剪切粘附力，并在Crazyflie四旋翼上成功演示了在平滑表面的可重复附着与快速分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电粘附技术为微型飞行机器人提供电控可切换的粘附能力，是实现平滑表面栖息的有前景机制。然而，用于空中栖息的柔软可拉伸电粘附垫的实际应用仍然有限，需要开发更实用的制造方法和验证其实际性能。

Method: 1) 开发了高效制造正弦波和同心圆电极的柔软可拉伸电粘附垫的工作流程；2) 使用Instron测试装置在0 kV和4.8 kV条件下对比法向和剪切粘附力；3) 在配备电粘附脚的Crazyflie四旋翼上进行栖息演示，测试在平面和曲面基板上的性能。

Result: 剪切粘附力占主导地位，在部分垫接触情况下可达约3 N；法向粘附力相对较小且强烈依赖于基板特性。Crazyflie原型机在平滑塑料表面（包括曲面几何形状）上实现了可重复附着，并在电压移除时快速分离。

Conclusion: 柔软电粘附脚为微型飞行器提供了轻量可靠的栖息解决方案，剪切粘附力显著优于法向粘附力，在平滑表面上表现出良好的附着性能和快速分离能力，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Electroadhesion (EA) provides electrically switchable adhesion and is a promising mechanism for perching micro aerial robots on smooth surfaces. However, practical implementations of soft and stretchable EA pads for aerial perching remain limited. This work presents (i) an efficient workflow for fabricating soft, stretchable electroadhesive pads with sinusoidal wave and concentric-circle electrodes in multiple sizes, (ii) a controlled experimental comparison of normal and shear adhesion under inactive (0 kV) and active (4.8 kV) conditions using an Instron-based setup, and (iii) a perching demonstration using a Crazyflie quadrotor equipped with electroadhesive feet on flat and curved substrates. Experimental results show that shear adhesion dominates, reaching forces on the order of 3 N with partial pad contact, while normal adhesion is comparatively small and strongly dependent on substrate properties. The Crazyflie prototype demonstrates repeatable attachment on smooth plastic surfaces, including curved geometries, as well as rapid detachment when the voltage is removed. These results highlight the potential of soft electroadhesive feet for lightweight and reliable perching in micro aerial vehicles (MAVs).

</details>


### [25] [Characterizing Lidar Range-Measurement Ambiguity due to Multiple Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09282)
*Jason H. Rife,Yifan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文分析了激光雷达在道路环境中可能产生概率性多回波的情况，并通过数据集分析提供了代表性累积分布函数，讨论了这对激光雷达定位的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光雷达在自动驾驶车辆位姿估计中应用日益广泛，但其在道路环境中的性能尚未被充分理解。现有定位算法通常假设激光雷达沿给定射线路径总能识别唯一表面，然而实际中当多个散射表面出现在激光雷达锥形光束内时，可能产生概率性测量。

Method: 分析激光雷达数据集以表征特定射线路径上出现概率性回波的情况，为两种不同机械旋转式激光雷达单元（基座固定）提供代表性累积分布函数，并提出定性方法来评估概率性多回波情况对激光雷达定位的影响。

Result: 通过数据分析识别了激光雷达在道路环境中产生概率性多回波的具体情况，建立了相关累积分布函数，为理解激光雷达测量不确定性提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 激光雷达在道路环境中确实存在概率性多回波现象，这对定位算法中"唯一表面"的假设构成挑战。提出的累积分布函数和定性评估方法有助于改进激光雷达定位系统的鲁棒性设计。

Abstract: Reliable position and attitude sensing is critical for highly automated vehicles that operate on conventional roadways. Lidar sensors are increasingly incorporated into pose-estimation systems. Despite its great utility, lidar is a complex sensor, and its performance in roadway environments is not yet well understood. For instance, it is often assumed in lidar-localization algorithms that a lidar will always identify a unique surface along a given raypath. However, this assumption is not always true, as ample prior evidence exists to suggest that lidar units may generate measurements probabilistically when more than one scattering surface appears within the lidar's conical beam. In this paper, we analyze lidar datasets to characterize cases with probabilistic returns along particular raypaths. Our contribution is to present representative cumulative distribution functions (CDFs) for raypaths observed by two different mechanically rotating lidar units with stationary bases. In subsequent discussion, we outline a qualitative methodology to assess the effect of probabilistic multi-return cases on lidar-based localization.

</details>


### [26] [A Benchmark of Dexterity for Anthropomorphic Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09294)
*Davide Liconti,Yuning Zhou,Yasunori Toshimitsu,Ronan Hinchet,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POMDAR是一个用于拟人机器人手的灵巧性基准测试，通过结构化任务集将灵巧性定义为可测量的任务性能，提供客观、可复现的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前拟人机器人手设计中"灵巧性"概念定义模糊且使用不一致，不同系统在不同标准下评估，导致难以进行有意义的跨设计比较，需要基于可测量结果的统一性能定义。

Method: 从人类运动控制分类学系统推导出结构化操作和抓取动作集，实现真实世界和仿真环境，包含四种操作配置（垂直、水平、连续旋转、纯抓取），任务设计包含机械支架约束运动、抑制补偿策略，定义结合任务正确性和执行速度的量化评分指标。

Result: 开发了POMDAR基准测试，提供开源、标准化、基于分类学的评估框架，能够客观、可复现、可解释地评估不同手设计，促进灵巧操作平台的系统化发展。

Conclusion: POMDAR为拟人机器人手提供了一致比较和评估的基准测试，将灵巧性形式化为可测量的任务性能，有助于推动灵巧操作技术的系统化进步。

Abstract: Dexterity is a central yet ambiguously defined concept in the design and evaluation of anthropomorphic robotic hands. In practice, the term is often used inconsistently, with different systems evaluated under disparate criteria, making meaningful comparisons across designs difficult. This highlights the need for a unified, performance-based definition of dexterity grounded in measurable outcomes rather than proxy metrics. In this work, we introduce POMDAR, a comprehensive dexterity benchmark that formalizes dexterity as task performance across a structured set of manipulation and grasping motions. The benchmark was systematically derived from established taxonomies in human motor control. It is implemented in both real-world and simulation and includes four manipulation configurations: vertical and horizontal configurations, continuous rotation, and pure grasping. The task designs contain mechanical scaffolding to constrain task motion, suppress compensatory strategies, and enable metrics to be measured unambiguously. We define a quantitative scoring metric combining task correctness and execution speed, effectively measuring dexterity as throughput. This enables objective, reproducible, and interpretable evaluation across different hand designs. POMDAR provides an open-source, standardized, and taxonomy-grounded benchmark for consistent comparison and evaluation of anthropomorphic robot hands to facilitate a systematic advancement of dexterous manipulation platforms. CAD, simulation files, and evaluation videos are publicly available at https://srl-ethz.github.io/POMDAR/.

</details>


### [27] [Robust Adaptive Backstepping Impedance Control of Robots in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09323)
*Reza Nazmara,Alap Kshirsagar,Jan Peters,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒自适应反步阻抗控制(RABIC)策略，用于接触丰富且不确定环境中的机器人操作，无需机器人动态参数即可处理外部干扰和未建模动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对机器人在接触丰富和不确定环境中操作时面临的挑战，需要一种能够处理系统耦合动态、外部干扰和未建模动态的控制策略，同时不依赖机器人动态参数的精确知识。

Method: 采用基于反步法的自适应阻抗控制方案作为内环跟踪参考阻抗模型；使用泰勒级数估计器估计系统动态，自适应估计器确定外力上界；通过稳定性分析证明系统的半全局实际有限时间稳定性。

Result: 通过移动机械臂仿真和Franka Emika Panda机器人实验验证，RABIC相比PD控制表现出更安全的性能，同时确保轨迹跟踪和力监测。

Conclusion: RABIC框架为未来耦合移动和固定串联机械臂的自适应和学习型阻抗控制研究提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a Robust Adaptive Backstepping Impedance Control (RABIC) strategy for robots operating in contact-rich and uncertain environments. The proposed control strategy considers the complete coupled dynamics of the system and explicitly accounts for key sources of uncertainty, including external disturbances and unmodeled dynamics, while not requiring the robot's dynamic parameters in implementation. We propose a backstepping-based adaptive impedance control scheme for the inner loop to track the reference impedance model. To handle uncertainties, we employ a Taylor series-based estimator for system dynamics and an adaptive estimator for determining the upper bound of external forces. Stability analysis demonstrates the semi-global practical finite-time stability of the overall system. To demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, a simulated mobile manipulator scenario and experimental evaluations on a real Franka Emika Panda robot were conducted. The proposed approach exhibits safer performance compared to PD control while ensuring trajectory tracking and force monitoring. Overall, the RABIC framework provides a solid basis for future research on adaptive and learning-based impedance control for coupled mobile and fixed serially linked manipulators.

</details>


### [28] [Multimodal Anomaly Detection for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09326)
*Guilherme Ribeiro,Iordanis Antypas,Leonardo Bizzaro,João Bimbo,Nuno Cruz Garcia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MADRI：一种用于人机协作异常检测的多模态特征重建框架，通过视觉特征向量、机器人内部传感器和场景图进行异常检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中确保安全性和可靠性需要及时检测可能导致系统故障或不安全行为的意外事件。虽然重建模型在人机交互中已被积极探索，但直接在特征向量上操作的方法仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出MADRI框架，首先将视频流转换为语义有意义的特征向量，然后执行基于重建的异常检测。此外，通过机器人内部传感器读数和场景图增强这些视觉特征向量，使模型能够捕获视觉环境中的外部异常和机器人内部的故障。

Result: 实验结果表明，仅基于视觉特征向量的重建就能有效检测异常，而结合其他模态进一步提高了检测性能，突显了多模态特征重建在人机协作中实现鲁棒异常检测的优势。

Conclusion: 多模态特征重建方法能够有效检测人机协作中的异常，结合视觉特征、机器人内部传感器和场景图信息可以显著提高异常检测的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Ensuring safety and reliability in human-robot interaction (HRI) requires the timely detection of unexpected events that could lead to system failures or unsafe behaviours. Anomaly detection thus plays a critical role in enabling robots to recognize and respond to deviations from normal operation during collaborative tasks. While reconstruction models have been actively explored in HRI, approaches that operate directly on feature vectors remain largely unexplored. In this work, we propose MADRI, a framework that first transforms video streams into semantically meaningful feature vectors before performing reconstruction-based anomaly detection. Additionally, we augment these visual feature vectors with the robot's internal sensors' readings and a Scene Graph, enabling the model to capture both external anomalies in the visual environment and internal failures within the robot itself. To evaluate our approach, we collected a custom dataset consisting of a simple pick-and-place robotic task under normal and anomalous conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that reconstruction on vision-based feature vectors alone is effective for detecting anomalies, while incorporating other modalities further improves detection performance, highlighting the benefits of multimodal feature reconstruction for robust anomaly detection in human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [29] [VAG: Dual-Stream Video-Action Generation for Embodied Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09330)
*Xiaolei Lang,Yang Wang,Yukun Zhou,Chaojun Ni,Kerui Li,Jiagang Zhu,Tianze Liu,Jiajun Lv,Xingxing Zuo,Yun Ye,Guan Huang,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAG是一个基于流匹配的双流框架，联合生成视频和动作，改善跨模态一致性，为机器人策略学习提供合成数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型需要大量人类遥操作数据，但收集任务特定演示成本高昂。现有世界模型不适合策略学习，因为它们不提供配对的动作轨迹；世界-动作模型缺乏视频-动作对齐，而两阶段流水线效率低且误差累积

Method: 提出VAG：统一的基于流匹配的双流框架，在视觉和语言条件下联合生成视频和动作。通过同步两个分支的去噪过程，并使用自适应3D池化机制将紧凑的全局视频上下文传递到动作分支，改善生成过程中的跨模态一致性

Result: 在模拟和真实世界环境中，VAG生成对齐的视频-动作对，具有竞争力的预测质量，支持可执行轨迹回放，并提供有用的合成预训练数据，改善下游策略泛化能力

Conclusion: VAG作为一个实用的世界-动作模型，具有为具身数据合成提供实用解决方案的潜力

Abstract: Recent advances in robot foundation models trained on large-scale human teleoperation data have enabled robots to perform increasingly complex real-world tasks. However, scaling these systems remains difficult because collecting task-specific demonstrations is expensive and labor-intensive. Synthetic data, especially generated videos, offer a promising direction, but existing World Models (WMs) are not directly suitable for policy learning since they do not provide paired action trajectories. World-Action (WA) models partially address this by predicting actions with visual outputs, yet often lack strong video-action alignment, while two-stage pipelines that generate video first and then infer actions introduce inefficiency and error accumulation. To address these limitations, we propose VAG, a unified flow-matching-based dual-stream framework that jointly generates video and action under visual and language conditioning. By synchronizing denoising in both branches and using an adaptive 3D pooling mechanism to transfer compact global video context to the action branch, VAG improves cross-modal consistency during generation. Across both simulated and real-world settings, VAG produces aligned video-action pairs with competitive prediction quality, supports executable trajectory replay, and provides useful synthetic pretraining data that improves downstream policy generalization, indicating its potential as a practical world-action model for embodied data synthesis.

</details>


### [30] [Musculoskeletal Motion Imitation for Learning Personalized Exoskeleton Control Policy in Impaired Gait](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09431)
*Itak Choi,Ilseung Park,Eni Halilaj,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于生理合理肌肉骨骼仿真和强化学习的设备无关框架，用于为健全和临床人群生成可扩展的个性化外骨骼辅助控制策略，无需大量物理试验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统下肢外骨骼控制策略设计需要大量数据收集或迭代优化，限制了临床人群的可及性，需要一种更高效、可扩展的个性化辅助方法。

Method: 结合生理合理肌肉骨骼仿真与强化学习，构建设备无关框架，生成外骨骼控制策略，无需任务特定调优，适用于健全和病理步态模型。

Result: 控制策略生成与实验验证一致的髋关节和踝关节辅助扭矩，降低代谢成本；对模拟受损步态模型，提供不对称、缺陷特异性辅助，改善能量效率和双侧运动对称性。

Conclusion: 生理合理肌肉骨骼仿真结合强化学习可作为跨健全和临床人群的个性化外骨骼控制的可扩展基础，消除对大量物理试验的需求。

Abstract: Designing generalizable control policies for lower-limb exoskeletons remains fundamentally constrained by exhaustive data collection or iterative optimization procedures, which limit accessibility to clinical populations. To address this challenge, we introduce a device-agnostic framework that combines physiologically plausible musculoskeletal simulation with reinforcement learning to enable scalable personalized exoskeleton assistance for both able-bodied and clinical populations. Our control policies not only generate physiologically plausible locomotion dynamics but also capture clinically observed compensatory strategies under targeted muscular deficits, providing a unified computational model of both healthy and pathological gait. Without task-specific tuning, the resulting exoskeleton control policies produce assistive torque profiles at the hip and ankle that align with state-of-the-art profiles validated in human experiments, while consistently reducing metabolic cost across walking speeds. For simulated impaired-gait models, the learned control policies yield asymmetric, deficit-specific exoskeleton assistance that improves both energetic efficiency and bilateral kinematic symmetry without explicit prescription of the target gait pattern. These results demonstrate that physiologically plausible musculoskeletal simulation via reinforcement learning can serve as a scalable foundation for personalized exoskeleton control across both able-bodied and clinical populations, eliminating the need for extensive physical trials.

</details>


### [31] [Adaptor: Advancing Assistive Teleoperation with Few-Shot Learning and Cross-Operator Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09462)
*Yu Liu,Yihang Yin,Tianlv Huang,Fei Yan,Yuan Xu,Weinan Hong,Wei Han,Yue Cao,Xiangyu Chen,Zipei Fan,Xuan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Adaptor：一个用于跨操作者意图识别的少样本框架，通过噪声注入和几何感知关键帧提取处理轨迹不确定性，结合意图专家和预训练视觉语言模型实现鲁棒的动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助遥操作通过共享控制提高效率，但不同操作者的习惯和专业知识差异导致高度异构的轨迹分布，这会破坏意图识别的稳定性，需要解决跨操作者泛化问题。

Method: Adaptor采用两阶段框架：1）预处理阶段通过噪声注入合成轨迹扰动来建模意图不确定性，并进行几何感知关键帧提取；2）策略学习阶段使用意图专家编码处理后的轨迹，并将其与预训练视觉语言模型上下文融合，条件化动作专家进行动作生成。

Result: 在真实世界和模拟基准测试中，Adaptor实现了最先进的性能，提高了成功率和效率。该方法在不同专业水平的操作者之间表现出低方差，证明了鲁棒的跨操作者泛化能力。

Conclusion: Adaptor框架通过处理轨迹不确定性和融合多模态信息，有效解决了辅助遥操作中跨操作者意图识别的挑战，实现了稳定且高效的共享控制。

Abstract: Assistive teleoperation enhances efficiency via shared control, yet inter-operator variability, stemming from diverse habits and expertise, induces highly heterogeneous trajectory distributions that undermine intent recognition stability. We present Adaptor, a few-shot framework for robust cross-operator intent recognition. The Adaptor bridges the domain gap through two stages: (i) preprocessing, which models intent uncertainty by synthesizing trajectory perturbations via noise injection and performs geometry-aware keyframe extraction; and (ii) policy learning, which encodes the processed trajectories with an Intention Expert and fuses them with the pre-trained vision-language model context to condition an Action Expert for action generation. Experiments on real-world and simulated benchmarks demonstrate that Adaptor achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving success rates and efficiency over baselines. Moreover, the method exhibits low variance across operators with varying expertise, demonstrating robust cross-operator generalization.

</details>


### [32] [SafeMind: A Risk-Aware Differentiable Control Framework for Adaptive and Safe Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09474)
*Zukun Zhang,Kai Shu,Mingqiao Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeMind：一种可微分的随机安全控制框架，将概率控制屏障函数与语义上下文理解和元自适应风险校准相结合，为四足机器人提供形式化安全保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的四足机器人控制器虽然表现出色，但在模型不确定性、感知噪声和非结构化接触条件下缺乏形式化安全保证，需要一种能够统一安全控制和语义理解的方法

Method: 通过可微分的随机安全控制框架，结合概率控制屏障函数、语义上下文编码和元自适应风险校准，使用方差感知屏障约束嵌入可微分二次规划，保持端到端训练的梯度流

Result: 在Unitree A1和ANYmal C上以200Hz部署，验证了12种地形类型、动态障碍物、形态扰动和语义定义任务，相比最先进的CBF、MPC和混合RL基线，安全违规减少3-10倍，能耗降低10-15%

Conclusion: SafeMind框架成功地将形式化安全保证与语义理解和自适应风险校准相结合，为四足机器人在复杂环境中的安全控制提供了有效解决方案，同时保持实时控制性能

Abstract: Learning-based quadruped controllers achieve impressive agility but typically lack formal safety guarantees under model uncertainty, perception noise, and unstructured contact conditions. We introduce SafeMind, a differentiable stochastic safety-control framework that unifies probabilistic Control Barrier Functions with semantic context understanding and meta-adaptive risk calibration. SafeMind explicitly models epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty through a variance-aware barrier constraint embedded in a differentiable quadratic program, thereby preserving gradient flow for end-to-end training. A semantics-to-constraint encoder modulates safety margins using perceptual or language cues, while a meta-adaptive learner continuously adjusts risk sensitivity across environments. We provide theoretical conditions for probabilistic forward invariance, feasibility, and stability under stochastic dynamics. SafeMind is deployed on Unitree A1 and ANYmal C at 200~Hz and validated across 12 terrain types, dynamic obstacles, morphology perturbations, and semantically defined tasks. Experiments show that SafeMind reduces safety violations by 3--10x and energy consumption by 10--15% relative to state-of-the-art CBF, MPC, and hybrid RL baselines, while maintaining real-time control performance.

</details>

### [33] [The Fast Lane Hypothesis: Von Economo Neurons Implement a Biological Speed-Accuracy Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09229)
*Esila Keskin*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 首次提出Von Economo神经元（VENs）的计算模型，验证"快速通道假说"：VENs通过稀疏快速投射通路实现生物速度-准确性权衡，支持快速社会决策而非表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VENs存在于具有复杂社会认知物种的前扣带回皮层和前额岛叶，在额颞叶痴呆中选择性缺失，在自闭症中发育异常，暗示其在快速社会决策中的作用，但此前缺乏计算模型解释其功能。

Method: 提出"快速通道假说"，将VENs建模为快速泄漏整合发放神经元（膜时间常数5ms，稀疏树突输入8个传入），与标准锥体神经元（20ms，80个传入）对比，构建包含2000个神经元的脉冲皮层电路，在社会辨别任务上训练。评估三种临床条件：典型（2% VENs）、自闭症样（0.4% VENs）和FTD样（训练后VEN消融）。

Result: 所有配置达到相同的渐近分类准确率（99.4%），支持VENs调节决策速度而非表征能力的预测。VENs产生的中位首次发放潜伏期比锥体神经元早4ms。在固定决策阈值下，典型条件显著快于FTD样条件，自闭症样条件介于两者之间。初步进化分析显示模型最优VEN比例与灵长类系统发育梯度定性对应。

Conclusion: 这是首个询问Von Economo神经元实际计算功能的计算模型，支持VENs通过稀疏快速通路实现速度-准确性权衡的假说，为理解其在快速社会决策中的作用提供了计算基础。

Abstract: Von Economo neurons (VENs) are large bipolar projection neurons found exclusively in the anterior cingulate cortex (ACC) and frontal insula of species with complex social cognition, including humans, great apes, and cetaceans. Their selective depletion in frontotemporal dementia (FTD) and altered development in autism implicate them in rapid social decision-making, yet no computational model of VEN function has previously existed. We introduce the Fast Lane Hypothesis: VENs implement a biological speed-accuracy tradeoff (SAT) by providing a sparse, fast projection pathway that enables rapid social decisions at the cost of deliberate processing accuracy. We model VENs as fast leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) neurons with membrane time constant 5 ms and sparse dendritic fan-in of eight afferents, compared to 20 ms and eighty afferents for standard pyramidal neurons, within a spiking cortical circuit of 2,000 neurons trained on a social discrimination task. Networks are evaluated under three clinically motivated conditions across 10 independent random seeds: typical (2% VENs), autism-like (0.4% VENs), and FTD-like (post-training VEN ablation). All configurations achieve equivalent asymptotic classification accuracy (99.4%), consistent with the prediction that VENs modulate decision speed rather than representational capacity. Temporal analysis confirms that VENs produce median first-spike latencies 4 ms earlier than pyramidal neurons. At a fixed decision threshold, the typical condition is significantly faster than FTD-like (t=-23.31, p<0.0001), while autism-like is intermediate (mean RT=26.91+/-9.01 ms vs. typical 20.70+/-2.02 ms; p=0.078). A preliminary evolutionary analysis shows qualitative correspondence between model-optimal VEN fraction and the primate phylogenetic gradient. To our knowledge, this is the first computational model that asks what a Von Economo neuron actually computes.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [34] [GNN-as-Judge: Unleashing the Power of LLMs for Graph Learning with GNN Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08553)
*Ruiyao Xu,Kaize Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GNN-as-Judge框架利用GNN的结构归纳偏置增强LLM在少样本半监督图学习中的性能，通过协作伪标签生成和弱监督微调解决标签稀缺和噪声问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在文本属性图上表现出色，但在低资源场景（标签节点严重受限）中性能受限，因为微调LLM通常需要足够的标注数据，特别是在图结构复杂时。主要挑战包括：1）为LLM生成和选择可靠伪标签的困难；2）使用伪标签微调LLM时需要缓解潜在标签噪声

Method: 提出GNN-as-Judge框架：1）协作伪标签策略：首先识别标注节点影响最大的未标注节点，然后利用LLM和GNN之间的一致性和分歧模式生成可靠标签；2）弱监督LLM微调算法：从信息丰富的伪标签中提取知识，同时缓解潜在标签噪声

Result: 在多个文本属性图数据集上的实验表明，GNN-as-Judge显著优于现有方法，特别是在标签数据稀缺的低资源场景中

Conclusion: 通过结合GNN的结构归纳偏置，GNN-as-Judge框架能够释放LLM在少样本半监督图学习中的潜力，有效解决伪标签生成和噪声缓解问题，为低资源图学习提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on text-attributed graphs (TAGs) due to their superior semantic understanding ability on textual node features. However, their effectiveness as predictors in the low-resource setting, where labeled nodes are severely limited and scarce, remains constrained since fine-tuning LLMs usually requires sufficient labeled data, especially when the TAG shows complex structural patterns. In essence, this paper targets two key challenges: (i) the difficulty of generating and selecting reliable pseudo labels on TAGs for LLMs, and (ii) the need to mitigate potential label noise when fine-tuning LLMs with pseudo labels. To counter the challenges, we propose a new framework, GNN-as-Judge, which can unleash the power of LLMs for few-shot semi-supervised learning on TAGs by incorporating the structural inductive bias of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). Specifically, GNN-as-Judge introduces a collaborative pseudo-labeling strategy that first identifies the most influenced unlabeled nodes from labeled nodes, then exploits both the agreement and disagreement patterns between LLMs and GNNs to generate reliable labels. Furthermore, we develop a weakly-supervised LLM fine-tuning algorithm that can distill the knowledge from informative pseudo labels while mitigating the potential label noise. Experiments on multiple TAG datasets demonstrate that GNN-as-Judge significantly outperforms existing methods, particularly in low-resource regimes where labeled data are scarce.

</details>


### [35] [Memory-Guided Trust-Region Bayesian Optimization (MG-TuRBO) for High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08569)
*Abhilasha Saroj,Shaked Regev,Guanhao Xu,Jinghui Yuan,Roy Luo,Ross Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较遗传算法与多种贝叶斯优化方法在交通仿真校准中的性能，提出记忆引导TuRBO方法在高维问题中表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通仿真和数字孪生校准是具有有限仿真预算的挑战性优化问题。每次试验都需要昂贵的仿真运行，且校准输入与模型误差之间的关系通常是非凸且带有噪声的。随着校准参数数量的增加，问题变得更加困难。

Method: 比较遗传算法(GA)与多种贝叶斯优化方法：经典贝叶斯优化(BO)、信任区域BO(TuRBO)、Multi-TuRBO和提出的记忆引导TuRBO(MG-TuRBO)。在2个真实世界交通仿真校准问题（14和84个决策变量）上评估性能。研究两种采集策略：汤普森采样和新的自适应策略。

Result: 在低维问题中，贝叶斯优化方法比遗传算法更快达到良好校准目标。MG-TuRBO在14D设置中表现相当，在84D问题中显示出明显优势，特别是与自适应策略结合使用时。

Conclusion: MG-TuRBO对于高维交通仿真校准特别有用，可能也适用于一般高维问题。贝叶斯优化方法在有限仿真预算下比遗传算法更有效。

Abstract: Traffic simulation and digital-twin calibration is a challenging optimization problem with a limited simulation budget. Each trial requires an expensive simulation run, and the relationship between calibration inputs and model error is often nonconvex, and noisy. The problem becomes more difficult as the number of calibration parameters increases. We compare a commonly used automatic calibration method, a genetic algorithm (GA), with Bayesian optimization methods (BOMs): classical Bayesian optimization (BO), Trust-Region BO (TuRBO), Multi-TuRBO, and a proposed Memory-Guided TuRBO (MG-TuRBO) method. We compare performance on 2 real-world traffic simulation calibration problems with 14 and 84 decision variables, representing lower- and higher-dimensional (14D and 84D) settings. For BOMs, we study two acquisition strategies, Thompson sampling and a novel adaptive strategy. We evaluate performance using final calibration quality, convergence behavior, and consistency across runs. The results show that BOMs reach good calibration targets much faster than GA in the lower-D problem. MG-TuRBO performs comparably in our 14D setting, it demonstrates noticeable advantages in the 84D problem, particularly when paired with our adaptive strategy. Our results suggest that MG-TuRBO is especially useful for high-D traffic simulation calibration and potentially for high-D problems in general.

</details>

*Ali Slim,Haydar Hamieh,Jawad Kotaich,Yehya Ghosn,Mahdi Chehimi,Ammar Mohanna,Hasan Abed Al Kader Hammoud,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuanBench+是一个统一的量子代码生成基准测试，涵盖Qiskit、PennyLane和Cirq三个框架，包含42个对齐任务，评估LLM在量子算法、门分解和状态准备等方面的代码生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子代码生成评估大多局限于单一框架，难以区分量子推理能力与框架熟悉度，需要跨框架的统一基准来准确评估LLM的量子代码生成能力。

Method: 创建QuanBench+基准，包含42个对齐任务；使用可执行功能测试评估模型；报告Pass@1和Pass@5；采用基于KL散度的概率输出接受标准；研究基于反馈的修复后的Pass@1（模型可在运行时错误或错误答案后修订代码）。

Result: 最强单次得分：Qiskit 59.5%、Cirq 54.8%、PennyLane 42.9%；基于反馈修复后最佳得分：Qiskit 83.3%、Cirq 76.2%、PennyLane 66.7%。

Conclusion: 量子代码生成取得明显进展，但可靠的多框架量子代码生成仍未解决，仍然严重依赖框架特定知识，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet quantum code generation is still evaluated mostly within single frameworks, making it difficult to separate quantum reasoning from framework familiarity. We introduce QuanBench+, a unified benchmark spanning Qiskit, PennyLane, and Cirq, with 42 aligned tasks covering quantum algorithms, gate decomposition, and state preparation.
  We evaluate models with executable functional tests, report Pass@1 and Pass@5, and use KL-divergence-based acceptance for probabilistic outputs. We additionally study Pass@1 after feedback-based repair, where a model may revise code after a runtime error or wrong answer. Across frameworks, the strongest one-shot scores reach 59.5% in Qiskit, 54.8% in Cirq, and 42.9% in PennyLane; with feedback-based repair, the best scores rise to 83.3%, 76.2%, and 66.7%, respectively. These results show clear progress, but also that reliable multi-framework quantum code generation remains unsolved and still depends strongly on framework-specific knowledge.

</details>


### [37] [Stability Enhanced Gaussian Process Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09331)
*Carl R. Richardson,Jichen Zhang,Ethan King,Ján Drgoňa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEGP-VAE模型，利用高维视频数据间接训练低维线性时不变系统，通过稳定增强的高斯过程先验结合概率模型与物理可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以从高维视频数据中学习低维线性时不变系统的稳定表示，存在数值不稳定和参数约束问题

Method: 提出稳定增强高斯过程变分自编码器，从LTI系统定义推导先验的均值和协方差函数，采用半收缩系统的完全无约束参数化限制搜索空间

Result: SEGP-VAE能够使用无约束优化算法训练，避免非Hurwitz状态矩阵引起的数值问题，在螺旋粒子视频数据集上实现准确的潜在状态预测

Conclusion: SEGP-VAE成功结合概率建模与物理可解释性，为从高维观测数据学习稳定低维动态系统提供有效框架

Abstract: A novel stability-enhanced Gaussian process variational autoencoder (SEGP-VAE) is proposed for indirectly training a low-dimensional linear time invariant (LTI) system, using high-dimensional video data. The mean and covariance function of the novel SEGP prior are derived from the definition of an LTI system, enabling the SEGP to capture the indirectly observed latent process using a combined probabilistic and interpretable physical model. The search space of LTI parameters is restricted to the set of semi-contracting systems via a complete and unconstrained parametrisation. As a result, the SEGP-VAE can be trained using unconstrained optimisation algorithms. Furthermore, this parametrisation prevents numerical issues caused by the presence of a non-Hurwitz state matrix. A case study applies SEGP-VAE to a dataset containing videos of spiralling particles. This highlights the benefits of the approach and the application-specific design choices that enabled accurate latent state predictions.

</details>


### [38] [Hierarchical Kernel Transformer: Multi-Scale Attention with an Information-Theoretic Approximation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08829)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HKT是一种多尺度注意力机制，通过可训练的因果下采样在L个分辨率级别处理序列，使用学习到的凸权重组合级别特定的得分矩阵，计算成本仅为标准注意力的4/3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种计算效率高的多尺度注意力机制，能够在不同分辨率级别捕捉序列模式，同时保持接近标准注意力的计算成本。

Method: 使用可训练的因果下采样在L个分辨率级别处理序列，每个级别生成特定的得分矩阵，通过学习到的凸权重组合这些矩阵，形成层次化得分矩阵。

Result: 在三个数据集上均优于重新训练的标准注意力基线：ListOps (+4.77pp)、sequential CIFAR-10 (+1.44pp)、IMDB字符级情感分析 (+7.47pp)，计算开销仅为1.31倍。

Conclusion: HKT提供了一种理论严谨的多尺度注意力框架，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升性能，严格包含单头标准注意力和因果卷积，并具有可解释的分解特性。

Abstract: The Hierarchical Kernel Transformer (HKT) is a multi-scale attention mechanism that processes sequences at L resolution levels via trainable causal downsampling, combining level-specific score matrices through learned convex weights. The total computational cost is bounded by 4/3 times that of standard attention, reaching 1.3125x for L = 3. Four theoretical results are established. (i) The hierarchical score matrix defines a positive semidefinite kernel under a sufficient condition on the symmetrised bilinear form (Proposition 3.1). (ii) The asymmetric score matrix decomposes uniquely into a symmetric part controlling reciprocal attention and an antisymmetric part controlling directional attention; HKT provides L independent such pairs across scales, one per resolution level (Propositions 3.5-3.6). (iii) The approximation error decomposes into three interpretable components with an explicit non-Gaussian correction and a geometric decay bound in L (Theorem 4.3, Proposition 4.4). (iv) HKT strictly subsumes single-head standard attention and causal convolution (Proposition 3.4). Experiments over 3 random seeds show consistent gains over retrained standard attention baselines: +4.77pp on synthetic ListOps (55.10+-0.29% vs 50.33+-0.12%, T = 512), +1.44pp on sequential CIFAR-10 (35.45+-0.09% vs 34.01+-0.19%, T = 1,024), and +7.47pp on IMDB character-level sentiment (70.19+-0.57% vs 62.72+-0.40%, T = 1,024), all at 1.31x overhead.

</details>


### [39] [Robust Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08571)
*Pavel Golikov,Evgenii Opryshko,Gennady Pekhimenko,Mark C. Jeffrey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出14种扰动技术评估LLM数学推理鲁棒性，发现开源模型在扰动下性能严重下降，并揭示密集注意力机制中中间推理步骤会污染工作记忆


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在标准数学基准测试中表现优异，但其推理过程严重过拟合于标准文本格式。需要评估LLM推理的鲁棒性，并深入理解其工作记忆机制中的缺陷

Method: 提出包含14种技术的扰动流水线，应用于AIME 2024数据集评估8个SOTA模型。通过强制模型在单个上下文窗口中顺序解决多个未扰动数学问题，严格隔离工作记忆容量
