Intent Lenses reify users' capture-time intent inferred from captured information into reusable interactive objects that encode the function to perform, the information sources to focus on, and how results are represented at an appropriate level of detail. These lenses are dynamically generated using the reasoning capabilities of large language models. To investigate this concept, we instantiate Intent Lenses in the context of academic conference photos and present an interactive system that infers lenses from presentation captures to generate structured visual notes on a spatial canvas. Users can further add, link, and arrange lenses across captures to support exploration and sensemaking. A study with nine academics showed that intent-mediated notes aligned with users' expectations, providing effective overviews of their captures while facilitating deeper sensemaking.

</details>


### [116] [Demonstrably Informed Consent in Privacy Policy Flows: Evidence from a Randomized Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09518)
*Qian Ma,Aditya Majumdar,Sarah Rajtmajer,Brett Frischmann*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过教学摩擦设计提升隐私政策知情同意质量，发现分页展示和节奏控制能提高理解度，但无门槛的同意流程中97.3%未达理解标准的用户仍会同意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前隐私政策同意流程通常只是长文档末尾的单一点击，缺乏真正的知情同意。隐私法学者提出"可证明的知情同意"标准，要求设计方提供用户理解关键条款的证据。本研究旨在探索如何通过教学摩擦设计在保持用户负担较低的同时支持可证明的理解。

Method: 采用随机实验设计，招募293名3-8岁儿童的父母，在六种不同条件下审查教育科技应用的隐私政策。条件包括不同展示格式（分页式、分段式）和节奏控制，其中三个条件为未通过理解测试的参与者提供第二次政策审查和重测机会。参与者需完成六题理解测试。

Result: 分页式条件（G3）首次尝试达标率最高（41.7%），其次是节奏控制的分段式条件（G4）（30.6%）。在重测条件下，64.9%的参与者在第二次尝试中提高了分数。关键发现：在未将同意与理解测试挂钩的条件下，97.3%得分低于阈值的参与者仍然选择同意，表明无门槛同意流程可能记录缺乏理解基础的同意。

Conclusion: 教学摩擦设计可以加强同意的证据基础，并明确其在时间和负担方面的成本。研究结果表明，通过适当的设计干预可以提高隐私政策的理解度，但当前无门槛的同意流程存在重大缺陷，可能记录缺乏真正理解的同意。

Abstract: Privacy policies govern how personal data is collected, used, and shared. Yet, in most privacy-policy consent flows, agreement is operationalized as a single click at the end of a long, opaque policy document. Recent privacy-law scholarship has argued for a standard of demonstrably informed consent. That is, the party drafting and designing privacy-policy consent mechanisms must generate reliable evidence that a person demonstrates comprehension of the consequential terms to which they agree. To this end, we study pedagogical friction as a design framing: minimal interventions embedded within a privacy-policy consent flow that aim to support demonstrated comprehension while keeping burden on the user low. In a randomized experiment, we tested pedagogical friction for demonstrably informed consent in the context of a privacy policy for an edtech app for young children. We recruited 293 parents of kids ages 3-8 to review the app's privacy policy under one of six conditions that varied presentation format and pacing, then complete a six-question comprehension quiz. Three conditions offered a second policy review and quiz retake for participants who did not pass this quiz on their first attempt. We find that the slide-based condition (G3) achieved the highest first-attempt threshold attainment (>=80%) (41.7%), followed by the paced, sectioned condition (G4) (30.6%). In the retake conditions, 64.9% of participants who completed a second attempt improved their score. Notably, in conditions that did not gate consent on demonstrated comprehension, 97.3% of participants who scored below the threshold still chose to consent, suggesting that ungated consent flows can record agreement without demonstrated comprehension. Our results suggest that pedagogical friction can strengthen the evidentiary basis of consent and clarify what it costs in time and burden.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Semantic Intent Fragmentation: A Single-Shot Compositional Attack on Multi-Agent AI Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08608)
*Tanzim Ahad,Ismail Hossain,Md Jahangir Alam,Sai Puppala,Yoonpyo Lee,Syed Bahauddin Alam,Sajedul Talukder*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SIF攻击利用LLM编排系统的任务分解特性，使单个合法请求分解为看似无害但组合后违反安全策略的子任务，绕过现有基于子任务的安全机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM编排系统的安全机制主要在子任务层面运行，无法检测组合后出现的策略违规。这种组合安全漏洞可能被恶意利用，需要系统性研究。

Method: 提出三阶段红队测试流程，基于OWASP、MITRE ATLAS和NIST框架构建现实企业场景。通过四种机制实现SIF攻击：批量范围升级、静默数据外泄、嵌入式触发器部署和准标识符聚合。

Result: 在14个企业场景中，GPT-20B编排器在71%情况下生成违反策略的计划，而所有子任务均显示为良性。更强的编排器成功率更高。计划级信息流跟踪结合合规评估可在执行前检测所有攻击。

Conclusion: SIF攻击暴露了LLM编排系统的组合安全漏洞，但通过计划级信息流跟踪和合规评估可以检测和预防此类攻击，表明组合安全差距是可弥补的。

Abstract: We introduce Semantic Intent Fragmentation (SIF), an attack class against LLM orchestration systems where a single, legitimately phrased request causes an orchestrator to decompose a task into subtasks that are individually benign but jointly violate security policy. Current safety mechanisms operate at the subtask level, so each step clears existing classifiers -- the violation only emerges at the composed plan. SIF exploits OWASP LLM06:2025 through four mechanisms: bulk scope escalation, silent data exfiltration, embedded trigger deployment, and quasi-identifier aggregation, requiring no injected content, no system modification, and no attacker interaction after the initial request. We construct a three-stage red-teaming pipeline grounded in OWASP, MITRE ATLAS, and NIST frameworks to generate realistic enterprise scenarios. Across 14 scenarios spanning financial reporting, information security, and HR analytics, a GPT-20B orchestrator produces policy-violating plans in 71% of cases (10/14) while every subtask appears benign. Three independent signals validate this: deterministic taint analysis, chain-of-thought evaluation, and a cross-model compliance judge with 0% false positives. Stronger orchestrators increase SIF success rates. Plan-level information-flow tracking combined with compliance evaluation detects all attacks before execution, showing the compositional safety gap is closable.

</details>


### [118] [Post-Quantum Cryptography-Based Bidirectional Authentication Key Exchange Protocol and Industry Applications: A Case Study of Instant Messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08612)
*Abel C. H. Chen,James W. H. Tung,Austin B. Y. Lin,Chin-Ling Chen,Ching-Chun Chang,Chin-Chen Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于后量子密码的ML-KEM双向认证密钥交换协议，引入结合PQC数字签名和KEM的双用途证书方案，并在即时通讯应用中验证其可行性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强ML-KEM的双向认证能力，解决后量子密码环境下同时满足认证和加密需求的问题

Method: 提出基于PQC的双向认证密钥交换协议，设计三种双用途证书方案（复合方案、催化剂方案、变色龙方案），在证书中同时包含PQC数字签名公钥和PQC KEM公钥

Result: 实验验证了不同证书配置下的密钥交换消息长度和计算时间，展示了在即时通讯等实际应用中的可行性

Conclusion: 提出的协议和证书方案能有效实现后量子密码环境下的双向认证和加密，为实际应用提供了可行的解决方案

Abstract: This study aims to enhance the bidirectional authentication capability of ML-KEM (Module-Lattice-Based Key-Encapsulation Mechanism) by proposing the post-quantum cryptography-based (PQC-based) bidirectional authentication key exchange protocol. Furthermore, it introduces dual-usage certificates combining PQC-based DSA (Digital Signature Algorithm) and PQC-based KEM, which include composite schemes, catalyst schemes, and chameleon schemes. These dual-usage certificates utilize the PQC-based DSA public key and PQC-based KEM public key within the certificate to meet the requirements for bidirectional authentication and encryption, enabling the negotiation of a shared secret key. During the experimental phase, the study validates and compares key exchange message lengths and computation times under different certificate configurations. Finally, instant messaging is presented as an industry application to demonstrate the practical implementation of the proposed protocol.

</details>


### [119] [Retrieval Augmented Classification for Confidential Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08628)
*Yeseul E. Chang,Rahul Kailasa,Simon Shim,Byunghoon Oh,Jaewoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出使用检索增强分类(RAC)进行机密文档分类，相比监督微调(FT)，RAC在类别不平衡和数据变化时更稳定，能减少参数级泄漏，并支持即时数据更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实工作环境中文档流入流出频繁，需要持续更新知识。传统监督微调方法在类别不平衡、数据变化和上下文长度限制下表现不稳定，且存在敏感信息泄露到模型权重中的风险。

Method: 提出基于检索增强分类(RAC)的机密文档分类方法。RAC通过外部向量存储进行相似性匹配来做出分类决策，保持敏感内容在外部存储中而非模型权重内。设计了RAC分类流程和评估方案，并与监督微调(FT)在WikiLeaks US Diplomacy语料库上进行对比实验。

Result: 在平衡数据上，RAC与FT表现相当。在不平衡数据上，RAC更稳定且性能相当：原始(不平衡)和增强(平衡)数据集上均达到约96%准确率，适当提示下F1可达94%；而FT在增强平衡集上达到90% F1，但在原始不平衡集上降至88% F1。RAC对标签偏斜不敏感，减少参数级泄漏，并能通过重新索引即时纳入新数据。

Conclusion: 当鲁棒增强不可行时，RAC提供了一种实用、安全保护的方法来实现强分类，将敏感内容保留在模型权重之外并处于控制之下。RAC在类别平衡、数据、上下文长度或治理要求等现实条件变化时保持鲁棒性，为受监管部署提供了可行的设计模式。

Abstract: Unauthorized disclosure of confidential documents demands robust, low-leakage classification. In real work environments, there is a lot of inflow and outflow of documents. To continuously update knowledge, we propose a methodology for classifying confidential documents using Retrieval Augmented Classification (RAC). To confirm this effectiveness, we compare RAC and supervised fine tuning (FT) on the WikiLeaks US Diplomacy corpus under realistic sequence-length constraints. On balanced data, RAC matches FT. On unbalanced data, RAC is more stable while delivering comparable performance--about 96% Accuracy on both the original (unbalanced) and augmented (balanced) sets, and up to 94% F1 with proper prompting--whereas FT attains 90% F1 trained on the augmented, balanced set but drops to 88% F1 trained on the original, unbalanced set. When robust augmentation is infeasible, RAC provides a practical, security-preserving path to strong classification by keeping sensitive content out of model weights and under your control, and it remains robust as real-world conditions change in class balance, data, context length, or governance requirements. Because RAC grounds decisions in an external vector store with similarity matching, it is less sensitive to label skew, reduces parameter-level leakage, and can incorporate new data immediately via reindexing--a difficult step for FT, which typically requires retraining. The contributions of this paper are threefold: first, a RAC-based classification pipeline and evaluation recipe; second, a controlled study that isolates class imbalance and context-length effects for FT versus RAC in confidential-document grading; and third, actionable guidance on RAC design patterns for governed deployments.

</details>


### [120] [Realisation-Level Privacy Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08630)
*Sophie Taylor,Praneeth Vippathalla,Justin Coon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于实现级别隐私泄露的差分隐私数据发布过滤方法，通过实时监控实际泄露而非最坏情况参数来优化效用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私组合定理和隐私过滤器基于每轮最坏情况隐私参数，存在优化空间，需要更精细的基于实际泄露的隐私核算方法

Method: 提出实现级别过滤方法，通过监控实际隐私泄露确定数据发布的停止时间，设计了一个能处理任意机制（包括在Rényi差分隐私下表现不佳的机制）的过滤器

Result: 证明了过滤器能保证(ε,δ)-差分隐私，数值证据表明实现级别过滤相比机制级别方法能提供更好的效用

Conclusion: 实现级别过滤为差分隐私数据发布提供了超越传统机制级别方法的效用提升路径，能处理更广泛的机制类型

Abstract: We study differentially private data release, where a database is accessed through successive, possibly adaptive queries and mechanisms. Existing composition theorems and privacy filters combine worst case per-round privacy parameters, leaving room for more refined accounting based on realised leakage, which we term realisation-level accounting. We propose a realisation-level filtering approach to determine stopping times for data releases, and design one such filter. Despite technical challenges arising from conditioning on realisations and stopping time, we prove that the filter guarantees $(ε, δ)$-differential privacy, with $ε$ and $δ$ chosen by the data handler. Through numerical evidence, we demonstrate that realisation-level filtering provides a path to better utility beyond mechanism-level methods. Furthermore, our proposed filter applies to arbitrary mechanisms, including those that are badly behaved under Rényi differential privacy.

</details>


### [121] [Follow My Eyes: Backdoor Attacks on VLM-based Scanpath Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08766)
*Diana Romero,Mutahar Ali,Momin Ahmad Khan,Habiba Farrukh,Fatima Anwar,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文首次研究了针对VLM（视觉语言模型）扫描路径预测的后门攻击，提出了两种可变输出攻击方法，并在移动设备上验证了实际威胁可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描路径预测模型在移动系统中用于驱动注视点渲染和基于注意力的交互，其安全性至关重要。目前缺乏对VLM扫描路径预测后门攻击的研究，需要评估这类攻击的威胁和防御方法。

Method: 设计了两种可变输出后门攻击：1）输入感知的空间攻击，将预测注视点重定向到攻击者选择的目标对象；2）扫描路径持续时间攻击，通过增加注视持续时间延迟视觉搜索完成。评估了三种触发方式（视觉、文本、多模态）、多种中毒比例和五种后训练防御方法。

Result: 发现朴素固定路径攻击虽然有效，但在连续输出空间中会产生可检测的聚类。设计的两种可变输出攻击能产生多样且合理的扫描路径，逃避基于聚类的检测。没有防御方法能同时在所有配置下抑制攻击并保持干净性能。后门行为在量化和部署到旗舰及老旧智能手机后依然存在。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对VLM扫描路径预测的后门攻击研究，证明了实际威胁可行性。现有防御方法无法有效应对这类攻击，需要开发更强大的安全机制来保护边缘部署的注视驱动系统。

Abstract: Scanpath prediction models forecast the sequence and timing of human fixations during visual search, driving foveated rendering and attention-based interaction in mobile systems where their integrity is a first-class security concern. We present the first study of backdoor attacks against VLM-based scanpath prediction, evaluated on GazeFormer and COCO-Search18. We show that naive fixed-path attacks, while effective, create detectable clustering in the continuous output space. To overcome this, we design two variable-output attacks: an input-aware spatial attack that redirects predicted fixations toward an attacker-chosen target object, and a scanpath duration attack that inflates fixation durations to delay visual search completion. Both attacks condition their output on the input scene, producing diverse and plausible scanpaths that evade cluster-based detection. We evaluate across three trigger modalities (visual, textual, and multimodal), multiple poisoning ratios, and five post-training defenses, finding that no defense simultaneously suppresses the attacks and preserves clean performance across all configurations. We further demonstrate that backdoor behavior survives quantization and deployment on both flagship and legacy commodity smartphones, confirming practical threat viability for edge-deployed gaze-driven systems.

</details>


### [122] [Stringology-Based Cryptanalysis for EChaCha20 Stream Cipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08862)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文使用字符串学方法（KMP和BM算法）分析EChaCha20流密码，验证其扩展6×6状态矩阵和增强QR-F函数是否引入可检测的统计模式，结果表明EChaCha20在16位和32位级别保持强伪随机性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管EChaCha20进行了设计增强，但尚未有系统研究验证其扩展的6×6状态矩阵和修改的Quarter-Round Function是否引入了微妙的密钥流模式、旋转偏差或部分碰撞，这些可能作为统计区分器。填补这一空白对于确保密码修改不会无意中降低其安全边际至关重要。

Method: 采用Knuth-Morris-Pratt (KMP)和Boyer-Moore (BM)算法分析EChaCha20，开发并优化了针对32位字级模式分析的算法适配版本，用于研究m位模式频率分布，评估EChaCha20对旋转差分攻击的抵抗能力。

Result: 实验结果表明：1) EChaCha20在16位和32位级别保持强伪随机性，仅在8位域观察到轻微不规则性；2) 差分测试显示快速扩散特性，经过两轮QR-F后表现出雪崩效应；3) 在评估范围内未观察到统计显著的旋转碰撞，与超过3轮后预期的ARX扩散行为一致。

Conclusion: 字符串学密码分析可作为ARX密码评估的补充工具，为EChaCha20的安全特性提供了新的见解，证实其修改设计未引入可检测的统计弱点。

Abstract: Stringology-Based Cryptanalysis (SBC) offers a suitable and a structurally aligned approach for uncovering structural patterns in stream ciphers that traditional statistical tests may often fail to detect. Despite \texttt{EChaCha20}'s design enhancements, no systematic investigation has been performed to determine whether its expanded 6$\times$6 state matrix and modified Quarter-Round Function (\texttt{QR-F}) introduce subtle keystream patterns, rotational biases, or partial collisions that could serve as statistical distinguishers. As such, addressing this gap is critical to ensure that the cipher's modifications do not unintentionally reduce its security margin. Therefore, this paper leverages Knuth-Morris-Pratt (\texttt{KMP}) and Boyer-Moore (\texttt{BM}) algorithms to analyze \texttt{EChaCha20}, which is a variant of ChaCha20 that features an expanded 6$\times$6 state matrix and an enhanced \texttt{QR-F}. The author has developed and optimized adaptations of the \texttt{KMP} and \texttt{BM} algorithms for 32-bit word level pattern analysis and employed them to investigate $m$-bit pattern frequency distributions to assess the \texttt{EChaCha20}'s resistance of rotational-differential attacks. Our experimental results on large-scale one million keystream datasets have confirmed that \texttt{EChaCha20} is able to maintain strong pseudorandomness at 16-bit and 32-bit levels with minor irregularities observed in the 8-bit domain. In addition to these, the differential tests have indicated a rapid diffusion, exhibiting an avalanche effect after two \texttt{QR-F} rounds and no statistically significant rotational collisions were observed within the evaluated bounds, consistent with expected ARX diffusion behavior beyond 3 rounds.
  This work puts forward SBC as a complementary tool for ARX cipher evaluation and provide new thoughts on the security properties of \texttt{EChaCha20}.

</details>


### [123] [CLIP-Inspector: Model-Level Backdoor Detection for Prompt-Tuned CLIP via OOD Trigger Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09101)
*Akshit Jindal,Saket Anand,Chetan Arora,Vikram Goyal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CLIP-Inspector (CI) 是一种针对提示调优CLIP模型的后门检测方法，通过重构可能的触发器来识别恶意提供商植入的后门，并能通过微调修复模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织将模型训练外包给MLaaS提供商时面临安全风险：恶意提供商可在遵循提示调优协议的同时植入后门，使触发输入被分类到攻击者选择的类别，且现有方法无法检测这种仅影响提示而不修改编码器的后门。

Method: CLIP-Inspector (CI) 假设对交付模型具有白盒访问权限，并拥有未标记的分布外图像池。CI为每个类别重构可能的触发器，以确定模型是否表现出后门行为。该方法还能利用重构的触发器对正确标记的触发输入进行微调，重新对齐模型。

Result: 在10个数据集和4种后门攻击的广泛实验中，CI仅使用1,000张分布外图像在单个epoch内就能重构有效触发器，达到94%的检测准确率（50个模型中检测出47个）。与适应的触发器反转基线相比，CI的AUROC分数显著更高（0.973 vs 0.495/0.687）。

Conclusion: CLIP-Inspector能够有效检测提示调优CLIP模型中的后门，并通过重构触发器进行微调来修复模型，确保安全部署。该方法解决了现有方法无法检测仅影响提示的后门的问题。

Abstract: Organisations with limited data and computational resources increasingly outsource model training to Machine Learning as a Service (MLaaS) providers, who adapt vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP to downstream tasks via prompt tuning rather than training from scratch. This semi-honest setting creates a security risk where a malicious provider can follow the prompt-tuning protocol yet implant a backdoor, forcing triggered inputs to be classified into an attacker-chosen class, even for out-of-distribution (OOD) data. Such backdoors leave encoders untouched, making them undetectable to existing methods that focus on encoder corruption. Other data-level methods that sanitize data before training or during inference, also fail to answer the critical question, "Is the delivered model backdoored or not?" To address this model-level verification problem, we introduce CLIP-Inspector (CI), a backdoor detection method designed for prompt-tuned CLIP models. Assuming white-box access to the delivered model and a pool of unlabeled OOD images, CI reconstructs possible triggers for each class to determine if the model exhibits backdoor behaviour or not. Additionally, we demonstrate that using CI's reconstructed trigger for fine-tuning on correctly labeled triggered inputs enables us to re-align the model and reduce backdoor effectiveness. Through extensive experiments across ten datasets and four backdoor attacks, we demonstrate that CI can reconstruct effective triggers in a single epoch using only 1,000 OOD images, achieving a 94% detection accuracy (47/50 models). Compared to adapted trigger-inversion baselines, CI yields a markedly higher AUROC score (0.973 vs 0.495/0.687), thus enabling the vetting and post-hoc repair of prompt-tuned CLIP models to ensure safe deployment.

</details>


### [124] [Hagenberg Risk Management Process (Part 3): Operationalization, Probabilities, and Causal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09153)
*Eckehard Hermann,Harald Lampesberger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Hagenberg风险管理流程，将定性Bowtie图转化为贝叶斯DAG模型，实现实时风险管理，并开发了Realtime Risk Studio工具和Probability Capture概率获取方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于无法接受、缓解或消除的风险，持续监控是可行方法，但需要可操作化的模型。现有Bowtie图是定性工具，无法支持实时风险管理和概率推断，需要桥接定性风险分析与实时风险管理之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 提出Hagenberg风险管理流程，将Bowtie图转化为适合贝叶斯推断的有向无环图(DAG)；2) 开发Realtime Risk Studio工具，添加显式安全状态语义，指定激活节点作为干预点；3) 开发Probability Capture工具，通过问卷自动生成从专家获取概率估计，使用描述性分散分析和先验正则化方法聚合条件概率评估。

Result: 1) 建立了从定性Bowtie图到概率DAG模型的转换框架；2) 实现了实时风险管理的可操作化模型；3) 提供了因果分析能力，包括d-分离、调整集检查、do-演算、局部独立性检查、证据更新和干预效果搜索；4) 通过即时支付网关场景验证了工作流程。

Conclusion: 论文贡献了一种方法和原型系统，将部分缓解的风险转化为可观察、概率化和可干预的模型，实现了从定性风险分析到实时风险管理的桥接，而非提供统计验证。

Abstract: For risks that cannot be accepted, sufficiently mitigated, or eliminated, continuous observation is a viable approach but requires a model that can be operationalized. The Hagenberg Risk Management Process bridges this gap between qualitative risk analysis, using contextualized polar heatmaps (triage), and realtime risk management by extending Bowtie diagrams into a formal probabilistic runtime model. We introduce Realtime Risk Studio, a domain-specific modeling tool that (i) transforms Bowtie structures (causes, top event, barriers, consequences) into a directed acyclic graph (DAG) suitable for Bayesian inference, (ii) adds explicit safe-state semantics, and (iii) designates Activation Nodes as intervention points. Bowtie models are qualitative; however, Bayesian inference requires actual probabilities. As a second contribution, we present Probability Capture, a tool that complements our Realtime Risk Studio by automatically generating questionnaires from a DAG model so experts can provide estimates. The tool analyzes disagreement and aggregates conditional-probability assessments using both descriptive dispersion analysis and prior-regularized methods. Causal analysis can then provide insights into the DAG model, for example, via d-separation, adjustment-set inspection, do-calculus for what-if analysis, local independence checks, evidence updating, and impact-oriented searches for effective interventions.
  This workflow is illustrated with an instant-payments gateway scenario, demonstrating (a) explicit safe-state semantics, (b) Bowtie-to-DAG operationalization, (c) probability capture with visible expert noise, and (d) causal what-if analysis on a transformed and enriched model. Rather than presenting a statistical validation, the paper contributes a method and prototype system that transforms partially mitigated risks into observable, probabilistic, and intervention-ready models.

</details>


### [125] [Unreal Thinking: Chain-of-Thought Hijacking via Two-stage Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09235)
*Wenhan Chang,Tianqing Zhu,Ping Xiong,Faqian Guan,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为TSBH的两阶段后门劫持方法，通过MRTS解决恶意思维链数据稀缺问题，实现对LLMs思维链的持久劫持攻击，同时保持下游输出稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs在需要思维链解释的场景中部署增加，攻击者可能通过操纵可观察的思维链来实施恶意行为。在开源权重生态系统中，这种操纵可以通过轻量级适配器实现，易于分发和附加到基础模型上。然而，持久性思维链劫持面临三个主要挑战：难以在连续长思维链输出序列中直接劫持思维链标记同时保持稳定下游输出、恶意思维链数据稀缺、以及朴素后门注入方法的不稳定性。

Method: 提出了两阶段后门劫持方法：1) 使用多重反向树搜索从提示-输出对中构建输出对齐的思维链，无需直接从对齐模型中引出恶意思维链；2) 两阶段后门劫持首先诱导触发器条件化的中间思维链与恶意输出之间的不匹配，然后在MRTS生成的思维链上微调模型，这些思维链与恶意输出具有较低的嵌入距离，从而确保更强的语义相似性。

Result: 在多个开源权重模型上的实验表明，该方法成功诱导了触发器激活的思维链劫持，同时在评估框架下保持了劫持状态与基线状态之间的可量化区分。作者还探索了基于推理的缓解方法，并发布了安全推理数据集以支持未来研究。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LLMs思维链劫持的安全风险，提出了有效的攻击方法并建立了评估框架。通过发布代码和数据集，为安全感知和可靠推理的未来研究提供了支持，强调了在部署需要思维链解释的LLMs时需要考虑这种新型攻击向量。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings where Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is interpreted by users. This creates a new safety risk: attackers may manipulate the model's observable CoT to make malicious behaviors. In open-weight ecosystems, such manipulation can be embedded in lightweight adapters that are easy to distribute and attach to base models. In practice, persistent CoT hijacking faces three main challenges: the difficulty of directly hijacking CoT tokens within one continuous long CoT-output sequence while maintaining stable downstream outputs, the scarcity of malicious CoT data, and the instability of naive backdoor injection methods. To address the data scarcity issue, we propose Multiple Reverse Tree Search (MRTS), a reverse synthesis procedure that constructs output-aligned CoTs from prompt-output pairs without directly eliciting malicious CoTs from aligned models. Building on MRTS, we introduce Two-stage Backdoor Hijacking (TSBH), which first induces a trigger-conditioned mismatch between intermediate CoT and malicious outputs, and then fine-tunes the model on MRTS-generated CoTs that have lower embedding distance to the malicious outputs, thereby ensuring stronger semantic similarity. Experiments across multiple open-weight models demonstrate that our method successfully induces trigger-activated CoT hijacking while maintaining a quantifiable distinction between hijacked and baseline states under our evaluation framework. We further explore a reasoning-based mitigation approach and release a safety-reasoning dataset to support future research on safety-aware and reliable reasoning. Our code is available at https://github.com/ChangWenhan/TSBH_official.

</details>


### [126] [Cross-Paradigm Models of Restricted Syndrome Decoding with Application to CROSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09292)
*Étienne Burle,Aleksei Udovenko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文将受限伴随式解码问题转化为码和格问题，评估了CROSS签名方案的安全性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受限伴随式解码问题的安全性，该问题是CROSS后量子签名方案的基础，CROSS是NIST第二轮候选方案

Method: 将ResSD问题转化为特定结构和范数小的向量问题，然后归约到码基问题（正则伴随式解码）和格基问题（最近向量问题、短/近向量列表）

Result: 成功将ResSD归约到码和格问题，扩大了攻击面，为ResSD安全性提供了新见解，并在缩减参数上进行了实验验证

Conclusion: ResSD问题可以归约到码和格问题，这增加了CROSS签名方案的攻击面，需要重新评估其安全性

Abstract: Restricted Syndrome Decoding (ResSD) is a variant of linear code decoding problem where each of the error's entries must belong to a fixed small set of values. This problem underlies the security of CROSS, a post-quantum signature scheme that is one of the Round 2 candidates of NIST's ongoing additional signatures call. We show that solutions to this problem can be deduced from vectors of a particular structure and a small norm in newly constructed codes, in both Hamming and Euclidean metrics. This allows us to reduce Restricted Syndrome Decoding to both code-based (Regular Syndrome Decoding) and lattice-based problems (Closest Vector Problem, List of Short/Close Vectors), increasing the attack surface and providing new insights into the security of ResSD. We evaluate our attacks on CROSS instances both theoretically and experimentally on reduced parameters.

</details>


### [127] [BadSkill: Backdoor Attacks on Agent Skills via Model-in-Skill Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09378)
*Guiyao Tie,Jiawen Shi,Pan Zhou,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BadSkill攻击：针对AI智能体生态系统中第三方技能嵌入模型的供应链后门攻击，通过模型微调在特定语义触发条件下激活恶意行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体生态系统依赖可安装技能扩展功能，其中一些技能将训练好的模型作为执行逻辑的一部分。这带来了供应链风险：第三方技能可能表面良性，但在其捆绑模型中隐藏恶意行为。现有研究未充分关注这种"模型在技能中"的威胁面

Method: 提出BadSkill后门攻击框架：攻击者发布看似良性的技能，其嵌入模型经过后门微调，仅在特定语义触发条件满足时激活隐藏恶意载荷。采用复合目标函数训练嵌入分类器，结合分类损失、基于边距的分离和毒化聚焦优化。在OpenClaw启发的模拟环境中评估，包含13个技能（8个触发任务+5个非触发控制技能）

Result: 在5个模型家族的8种架构（494M-7.1B参数）上，BadSkill在8个触发技能上达到最高99.5%的平均攻击成功率，同时在负类查询上保持强良性准确率。在标准测试分割的毒化率扫描中，仅3%毒化率即可实现91.7%攻击成功率。攻击在评估的模型规模和五种文本扰动类型下均保持有效

Conclusion: 研究揭示了智能体生态系统中模型承载技能作为独特的模型供应链风险，需要更强的来源验证和行为审查机制来应对第三方技能工件的安全威胁

Abstract: Agent ecosystems increasingly rely on installable skills to extend functionality, and some skills bundle learned model artifacts as part of their execution logic. This creates a supply-chain risk that is not captured by prompt injection or ordinary plugin misuse: a third-party skill may appear benign while concealing malicious behavior inside its bundled model. We present BadSkill, a backdoor attack formulation that targets this model-in-skill threat surface. In BadSkill, an adversary publishes a seemingly benign skill whose embedded model is backdoor-fine-tuned to activate a hidden payload only when routine skill parameters satisfy attacker-chosen semantic trigger combinations. To realize this attack, we train the embedded classifier with a composite objective that combines classification loss, margin-based separation, and poison-focused optimization, and evaluate it in an OpenClaw-inspired simulation environment that preserves third-party skill installation and execution while enabling controlled multi-model study. Our benchmark spans 13 skills, including 8 triggered tasks and 5 non-trigger control skills, with a combined main evaluation set of 571 negative-class queries and 396 trigger-aligned queries. Across eight architectures (494M--7.1B parameters) from five model families, BadSkill achieves up to 99.5\% average attack success rate (ASR) across the eight triggered skills while maintaining strong benign-side accuracy on negative-class queries. In poison-rate sweeps on the standard test split, a 3\% poison rate already yields 91.7\% ASR. The attack remains effective across the evaluated model scales and under five text perturbation types. These findings identify model-bearing skills as a distinct model supply-chain risk in agent ecosystems and motivate stronger provenance verification and behavioral vetting for third-party skill artifacts.

</details>


### [128] [XFED: Non-Collusive Model Poisoning Attack Against Byzantine-Robust Federated Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09489)
*Israt Jahan Mouri,Muhammad Ridowan,Muhammad Abdullah Adnan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个无需攻击者间通信的非共谋模型投毒攻击XFED，该攻击在联邦学习中有效绕过现有防御机制，表明FL系统安全性比预期更脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习模型投毒攻击大多依赖攻击者间的共谋协调，这在实际部署中成本高且易被检测。研究旨在探索无需攻击者间通信的非共谋攻击是否可行，以揭示FL系统真实安全风险。

Method: 提出非共谋攻击模型，攻击者共享对抗目标但独立操作，不进行通信或获取其他客户端更新。开发了XFED攻击方法，该方法与聚合机制无关，每个攻击者独立生成恶意更新。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，XFED成功绕过八种最先进的防御机制，并优于六种现有模型投毒攻击，证明了非共谋攻击的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 联邦学习系统安全性远低于预期，非共谋模型投毒攻击构成严重威胁，亟需开发更鲁棒且实用的防御机制来应对这一新威胁模型。

Abstract: Model poisoning attacks pose a significant security threat to Federated Learning (FL). Most existing model poisoning attacks rely on collusion, requiring adversarial clients to coordinate by exchanging local benign models and synchronizing the generation of their poisoned updates. However, sustaining such coordination is increasingly impractical in real-world FL deployments, as it effectively requires botnet-like control over many devices. This approach is costly to maintain and highly vulnerable to detection. This context raises a fundamental question: Can model poisoning attacks remain effective without any communication between attackers? To address this challenge, we introduce and formalize the \textbf{non-collusive attack model}, in which all compromised clients share a common adversarial objective but operate independently. Under this model, each attacker generates its malicious update without communicating with other adversaries, accessing other clients' updates, or relying on any knowledge of server-side defenses. To demonstrate the feasibility of this threat model, we propose \textbf{XFED}, the first aggregation-agnostic, non-collusive model poisoning attack. Our empirical evaluation across six benchmark datasets shows that XFED bypasses eight state-of-the-art defenses and outperforms six existing model poisoning attacks. These findings indicate that FL systems are substantially less secure than previously believed and underscore the urgent need for more robust and practical defense mechanisms.

</details>


### [129] [Trans-RAG: Query-Centric Vector Transformation for Secure Cross-Organizational Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09541)
*Yu Liu,Kun Peng,Wenxiao Zhang,Fangfang Yuan,Cong Cao,Wenxuan Lu,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Trans-RAG：一种跨组织RAG系统，通过向量空间语言范式实现安全、准确、高效的检索增强生成，避免明文暴露和解密开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨组织部署的RAG系统面临安全、准确性和效率之间的根本矛盾。现有加密方法在解密时会暴露明文，而联邦架构既无法整合资源又带来巨大开销。

Method: 提出Trans-RAG系统，采用向量空间语言范式，每个组织的知识存在于数学上隔离的语义空间。核心是vector2Trans多阶段变换技术，使查询能够通过查询中心变换动态"说"每个组织的向量空间"语言"，消除解密开销同时保持原生检索效率。

Result: 安全评估显示向量空间接近正交（89.90°角分离）和99.81%的隔离率。在8个检索器、3个数据集和3个LLM上的实验表明，准确性下降最小（nDCG@10下降3.5%），相比同态加密有显著效率提升。

Conclusion: Trans-RAG通过创新的向量空间语言范式解决了跨组织RAG系统的安全、准确性和效率矛盾，为安全的多组织知识检索提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) systems deployed across organizational boundaries face fundamental tensions between security, accuracy, and efficiency. Current encryption methods expose plaintext during decryption, while federated architectures prevent resource integration and incur substantial overhead. We introduce Trans-RAG, implementing a novel vector space language paradigm where each organization's knowledge exists in a mathematically isolated semantic space. At the core lies vector2Trans, a multi-stage transformation technique that enables queries to dynamically "speak" each organization's vector space "language" through query-centric transformations, eliminating decryption overhead while maintaining native retrieval efficiency. Security evaluations demonstrate near-orthogonal vector spaces with 89.90° angular separation and 99.81% isolation rates. Experiments across 8 retrievers, 3 datasets, and 3 LLMs show minimal accuracy degradation (3.5% decrease in nDCG@10) and significant efficiency improvements over homomorphic encryption.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Systematic API Testing Through Model Checking and Executable Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08633)
*Ana Ribeiro,Margarida Mamede,Carla Ferreira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IcePick框架利用TLA+形式化建模和TLC模型检查实现API状态空间系统覆盖，结合Glacier合约语言解决语义验证问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有API黑盒测试依赖接口规范但缺乏行为语义，导致测试预言问题和状态化调用序列生成受限

Method: 使用TLA+形式化建模API状态演化，TLC模型检查器探索可达状态，覆盖引导的广度优先遍历缓解状态爆炸，Glacier一阶逻辑合约语言增强语义验证

Result: 在EvoMaster基准系统上实现完全状态覆盖，发现多操作交互故障，分析可扩展性以确定实际应用限制

Conclusion: IcePick为关键API系统提供可重复测试套件和强覆盖保证，通过形式化方法解决API测试的语义缺口问题

Abstract: Automated black-box testing of APIs typically relies on interface specifications that define available operations and data schemas, but offer limited or no behavioural semantics. This semantic gap amplifies the test-oracle problem and limits the generation of effective, stateful call sequences.
  We introduce IcePick, a framework that achieves systematic state-space coverage for API testing by leveraging model checking. IcePick uses TLA+ to formally model API state evolution, employs the TLC model checker to exhaustively explore reachable states, and generates test sequences that provably cover the behavioural model. To mitigate state-space explosion and improve sequence extraction, we introduce a coverage-guided breadth-first traversal of the TLC state-space graph. To address oracle limitations beyond HTTP status codes, we propose Glacier, a first-order logic contract language that enriches API specifications with executable semantic contracts, enabling automated behavioural verification during test execution.
  We evaluate IcePick on EvoMaster Benchmark systems, demonstrating that model-checking-guided exploration achieves complete state coverage and reveals faults in multi-operation interactions. We also analyse scalability to characterise practical limits and applicability requirements. Overall, IcePick provides reproducible test suites with strong coverage guarantees for critical API-based systems.

</details>


### [131] [An Eye for Trust: An Exploration of Developers' Trust Perceptions Through Urgency and Reputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08713)
*Sara Yabesi,Mahta Amini,Jelena Ristic,Zohreh Sharafi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过眼动追踪实验发现，代码优先级（紧急程度）显著影响开发者的代码审查行为，但作者声誉（资深vs初级）仅影响视觉扫描模式，两者均不影响最终的代码采用决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码重用是软件开发中的普遍实践，表明开发者对重用代码存在固有信任。然而，目前缺乏对开发者信任机制的基本理解，以及各种因素如何塑造其基于信任的认知过程。本研究旨在探索紧急程度（代码优先级）和声誉（作者经验水平）如何影响开发者对代码可信度的感知。

Method: 采用首个受控实验（n=37），结合眼动追踪技术，研究代码优先级（紧急程度）和作者经验水平（声誉）对开发者代码审查行为的影响。实验通过标记代码补丁的优先级（高/低）和作者级别（资深/初级）来操纵这两个变量。

Result: 1. 代码优先级显著影响开发者的代码审查行为，包括评估时间、认知负荷和感知质量；2. 作者声誉影响视觉代码扫描模式和注意力分布，但无显著性能差异；3. 代码采用决策不受优先级或声誉影响；4. 参与者将代码功能性、质量和可理解性列为主要评估因素；5. 参与者未意识到紧急程度和声誉对其审查和重用决策的实质性影响。

Conclusion: 尽管代码审查行为受到紧急程度和声誉的影响，但开发者最终是否采用代码的决策并未受这些因素影响。参与者未能意识到这些因素对其决策过程的影响。本研究为理解软件工程中的信任机制迈出了重要一步，可为代码审查平台设计、代码重用实践和自动化代码生成提供参考。

Abstract: Code reuse is a widespread practice across software development projects, suggesting an inherent trust in the reused code. Yet, there is a lack of a fundamental understanding of developers' trust and how various factors mold their trust-based cognitive processes. Drawing from the psychology of compliance and trust, we present the results of the first controlled experiment (n=37) which uses eye tracking to explore how urgency (represented by code priority level) and reputation (represented by the experience level of the code's author) influence developers' perceptions of code trustworthiness.
  Our research revealed that the priority assigned to a code patch significantly influenced developers' code review behavior, impacting their evaluation time, cognitive load, and perceived quality. However, the decision to incorporate and implement the code was not affected . Eye tracking data revealed that there were variations in overall visual code scanning and the distribution of attention across identical code patches labeled as written by senior vs. junior developers. Yet, there were no significant performance differences. Moreover, our participants nominate code functionality, quality, and comprehensibility as primary factors in code evaluation.
  Despite noticeable changes in code review behavior, our participants surprisingly overlooked the substantial influence of urgency and reputation on their decisions to review and reuse code changes. This study takes the next step toward a better understanding of trust in software engineering and may inform future research about code review platforms and guidelines, code reuse, and automated code generation.

</details>


### [132] [Demystifying the Silence of Correctness Bugs in PyTorch Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08720)
*Meiziniu Li,Dongze Li,Jianmeng Liu,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对PyTorch编译器(torch.compile)中的正确性bug进行实证研究，分析了这些bug的特征，评估了现有模糊测试工具的检测效果，并提出了一种专门检测此类bug的测试技术AlignGuard。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: torch.compile作为深度学习模型（包括大语言模型）的核心优化工具，存在导致编译后模型输出错误而不触发异常、崩溃或警告的正确性bug。这些bug对下游LLM应用的可靠性构成严重威胁，但尚未有系统研究专门表征和检测这些bug。

Method: 1. 对torch.compile中的正确性bug进行首次实证研究，分析其特征；2. 评估现有模糊测试工具检测这些bug的效果；3. 基于研究发现提出AlignGuard测试技术，该技术结合了从实证研究中提取的bug特征，并应用基于LLM的测试变异来检测正确性bug。

Result: 1. PyTorch社区数据显示，19.2%的高优先级问题是torch.compile bug导致的编译后DL模型输出错误；2. AlignGuard成功检测到23个新的正确性bug，所有bug均被PyTorch开发团队确认或修复，其中超过一半（14/23）被标记为高优先级bug。

Conclusion: torch.compile中的正确性bug是一个严重但未被充分研究的问题。AlignGuard作为一种专门针对此类bug的测试技术，展示了显著的有效性，能够检测出大量高优先级的正确性bug，为AI基础设施的可靠性提供了重要保障。

Abstract: Performance optimization of AI infrastructure is key to the fast adoption of large language models (LLMs). The PyTorch compiler (torch.compile), a core optimization tool for deep learning (DL) models (including LLMs), has received due attention. However, torch.compile is prone to correctness bugs, which cause incorrect outputs of compiled DL models without triggering exceptions, crashes, or warnings. These bugs pose a serious threat to the reliability of downstream LLM applications. Data from the PyTorch community shows that 19.2% of high-priority issues are incorrect outputs of compiled DL models induced by torch.compile bugs, the second-most-common bug category (only behind program crashes at 19.57%). However, no systematic study has been conducted to specifically characterize and thereby detect these bugs. In this paper, we present the first empirical study of the correctness bugs in torch.compile, examine their characteristics, and assess the effectiveness of existing fuzzers in detecting them. Based on our findings, we propose a proof-of-concept testing technique named AlignGuard, tailored specifically for detecting correctness bugs in torch.compile. AlignGuard incorporates bug characteristics distilled from our empirical study, applying LLM-based test mutation to existing test cases for correctness bug detection. At the time of writing, AlignGuard has successfully detected 23 new correctness bugs in recent torch.compile. All these bugs have been confirmed or fixed by the PyTorch development team, and over half (14/23) of them are even marked as high-priority bugs, underscoring the usefulness of our technique.

</details>


### [133] [A Longitudinal Study of Dependency Reclassifications in JavaScript Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08747)
*Yuxin Liu,Cristian Bogdan,Benoit Baudry*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究分析了JavaScript项目中依赖项重新分类（移除和角色重新分配）的普遍性，发现79.1%的项目会进行依赖项重新分类，其中97.2%会移除依赖项，38.0%会进行角色重新分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注依赖版本更新，而对开发者在项目演化过程中如何为依赖项分配不同角色（如运行时依赖、开发依赖、对等依赖）的变化模式了解甚少。本研究旨在探究JavaScript项目中依赖项重新分类（包括移除和角色重新分配）的实践情况。

Method: 分析了33,087个具有活跃依赖维护的JavaScript项目，研究开发者如何重新分类其依赖项，包括移除依赖项和在Core（运行时）、Dev（仅开发）和Peer（消费者提供）等不同角色之间重新分配。

Result: 依赖项重新分类是普遍的维护活动：79.1%的项目会进行依赖项重新分类。其中97.2%的项目会移除依赖项，38.0%的项目会进行角色重新分配。33.1%的项目后来会重新引入已移除的依赖项，11.2%的项目表现出重复的角色切换。重新分类实践通常持续较长时间，中位持续时间为408天。

Conclusion: 依赖项重新分类是JavaScript项目中普遍且重要的维护活动，其模式比之前认为的更为复杂和动态。这些发现扩展了依赖维护的研究范围，对依赖工具、包管理器和研究具有重要启示，有助于支持正确的依赖角色声明。

Abstract: Modern software projects depend on third-party dependencies, whose declarations must be maintained as projects evolve. Prior work has focused on dependency version updates, while much less is known about how developers assign dependencies to different roles over time. In this paper, we investigate how developers of JavaScript projects reclassify their dependencies, including removal and role reassignment. Our analysis of 33,087 JavaScript projects with active dependency maintenance reveals that dependency reclassification is a prevalent maintenance activity, occurring in 79.1% of the studied projects. Of these projects, nearly all (97.2%) remove dependencies at some point, while 38.0% undergo role reassignments across Core (runtime), Dev (development-only), and Peer (consumer-provided) roles. These changes are not always final, as 33.1% of projects later reintroduce removed dependencies and 11.2% exhibit repeated role switching. Reclassification practices also tend to unfold over long periods, with a median duration of 408 days. These findings broaden the study of dependency maintenance beyond version updates, and contribute implications for dependency tools, package managers, and research to support correct dependency role declarations.

</details>


### [134] [Real-Time Toxicity Filtering for Open-Source Code Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08886)
*Md Awsaf Alam Anindya,Showvik Biswas,Anindya Iqbal,Jaydeb Sarker,Amiangshu Bosu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ToxiShield是一个实时浏览器扩展，通过毒性识别、多分类和代码审查去毒化三个模块来识别和净化有毒的代码审查，旨在促进开源社区更包容的协作环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件开发中的有毒互动会损害社区协作，需要一种实时解决方案来识别和净化有毒的代码审查，以营造更包容的开源环境。

Method: 提出ToxiShield框架，包含三个模块：1) 基于微调BERT的二元毒性分类器；2) 使用Claude 3.5 Sonnet进行提示工程的多类别分类；3) 基于微调Llama 3.2的代码审查去毒化模型。

Result: 二元分类器在38,761个代码审查文本上达到97% F1分数；多类别分类在1,200个样本上达到39% MCC和42% F1；去毒化模型达到95.27%风格转移准确率、97.03%流畅度、67.07%内容保持率和84% J分数。10名开发者的验证表明ToxiShield有效。

Conclusion: ToxiShield通过实时识别和净化有毒代码审查，能够有效促进开源软件开发中更包容的协作环境，为社区健康提供实用工具。

Abstract: Toxic interactions in open-source software development harm community collaboration. To combat this, we propose ToxiShield, a realtime browser extension that identifies and detoxifies toxic code reviews. The framework comprises three modules: toxicity identification, reasoned multiclass classification, and code review detoxification. Our fine-tuned BERT-based binary classifier achieved a 97% F1-score on 38,761 code review texts. For multiclass classification, Claude 3.5 Sonnet with prompt engineering achieved a 39% MCC and 42% F1 on 1,200 samples. Finally, our fine-tuned Llama 3.2 detoxification model reached 95.27% style transfer accuracy, 97.03% fluency, 67.07% content preservation, and an 84% J-score. Validation with 10 software developers suggests ToxiShield effectively fosters a more inclusive open-source environment.

</details>


### [135] [Dissecting Bug Triggers and Failure Modes in Modern Agentic Frameworks: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08906)
*Xiaowen Zhang,Hannuo Zhang,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统分析了现代自主多智能体框架（如CrewAI和AutoGen）的可靠性问题，通过分析409个已修复bug，提出了五层抽象模型，揭示了自主编排特有的bug症状和根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体框架已演变为复杂的自主多智能体系统，引入了早期流水线式LLM库所没有的独特可靠性挑战。现有实证研究主要关注早期LLM库或任务级bug，尚未探索这些智能体框架的独特复杂性。

Method: 1. 收集并分析5个代表性智能体框架的409个已修复bug；2. 提出五层抽象模型捕捉智能体框架的结构复杂性（从编排层到基础设施层）；3. 识别智能体特有的bug症状和根本原因；4. 进行统计分析揭示跨框架一致性和bug维度间的显著关联；5. 通过自动化模式挖掘识别频繁触发bug的模式。

Result: 1. 发现了自主编排特有的bug症状，如意外执行序列和用户配置被忽略；2. 识别了智能体特有的根本原因，包括模型相关故障、认知上下文管理不当和编排故障；3. 统计分析显示跨框架一致性和bug维度间的显著关联；4. 自动化模式挖掘识别了频繁触发bug的模式（如模型后端-ID组合），并证明这些模式在不同框架设计间具有可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了智能体框架可靠性研究的空白，提出的五层抽象模型和发现的bug模式有助于跨平台测试，提高智能体系统的可靠性。研究成果为智能体框架的开发和测试提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Modern agentic frameworks (e.g., CrewAI and AutoGen) have evolved into complex, autonomous multi-agent systems, introducing unique reliability challenges beyond earlier pipeline-based LLM libraries. However, existing empirical studies focus on earlier LLM libraries or task-level bugs, leaving the unique complexities of these agentic frameworks unexplored. We bridge the gap by conducting a comprehensive study of 409 fixed bugs from five representative agentic frameworks. We propose a five-layer abstraction to capture structural complexities in agentic frameworks, spanning from orchestration to infrastructure. Our study uncovers specialized symptoms, such as unexpected execution sequences and user configurations ignored, which are unique to autonomous orchestration. We further identify agent-specific root causes, including modelrelated faults, cognitive context mismanagement, and orchestration faults. Statistical analysis reveals cross-framework consistency and significant associations among these bug dimensions. Finally, our automated pattern mining identifies frequent bug-triggering patterns (e.g., model backend-ID combinations), and we show their transferability across different framework designs. Our findings facilitate cross-platform testing and improve the reliability of agentic systems.

</details>


### [136] [DeepGuard: Secure Code Generation via Multi-Layer Semantic Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09089)
*Li Huang,Zhongxin Liu,Yifan Wu,Tao Yin,Dong Li,Jichao Bi,Nankun Mu,Hongyu Zhang,Meng Yan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeepGuard通过聚合多层表示来缓解代码生成LLMs的安全漏洞复制问题，相比仅使用最终层的监督方法，平均提升11.9%的安全正确生成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成LLMs容易从训练数据中复制不安全模式。常见的安全强化策略（基于最终transformer层的监督）存在"最终层瓶颈"问题：漏洞判别线索可能分布在多个层中，在接近输出表示时变得难以检测。

Method: 1) 通过分层线性探测诊断漏洞信号分布；2) 提出DeepGuard框架：使用基于注意力的模块聚合多个上层表示，利用分布式安全相关线索；3) 采用多目标训练目标平衡安全增强和功能正确性；4) 支持轻量级推理时引导策略。

Result: 在五个代码LLMs上的实验表明，DeepGuard相比SVEN等强基线，平均提升11.9%的安全正确生成率。同时保持功能正确性，并对未见漏洞类型具有泛化能力。

Conclusion: DeepGuard通过利用多层分布式安全线索有效缓解了代码生成LLMs的安全漏洞复制问题，相比仅依赖最终层的方法显著提升了安全性和正确性平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) for code generation can replicate insecure patterns from their training data. To mitigate this, a common strategy for security hardening is to fine-tune models using supervision derived from the final transformer layer. However, this design may suffer from a final-layer bottleneck: vulnerability-discriminative cues can be distributed across layers and become less detectable near the output representations optimized for next-token prediction. To diagnose this issue, we perform layer-wise linear probing. We observe that vulnerability-related signals are most detectable in a band of intermediate-to-upper layers yet attenuate toward the final layers. Motivated by this observation, we introduce DeepGuard, a framework that leverages distributed security-relevant cues by aggregating representations from multiple upper layers via an attention-based module. The aggregated signal powers a dedicated security analyzer within a multi-objective training objective that balances security enhancement and functional correctness, and further supports a lightweight inference-time steering strategy. Extensive experiments across five code LLMs demonstrate that DeepGuard improves the secure-and-correct generation rate by an average of 11.9% over strong baselines such as SVEN. It also preserves functional correctness while exhibiting generalization to held-out vulnerability types. Our code is public at https://github.com/unknownhl/DeepGuard.

</details>


### [137] [The Role of LLMs in Collaborative Software Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09120)
*Victoria Jackson,Yoonha Cha,Rafael Prikladnicki,André van der Hoek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究探索大型语言模型如何影响软件工程中的协作设计工作，发现LLM引入导致不同的联合使用模式，专业人员对LLM的依赖程度差异很大，但都会仔细审查和反思LLM的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究关注LLM在单独开发任务（如编码）中的应用，但对于LLM如何影响软件工程中的协作团队工作知之甚少。本研究聚焦于软件设计这一协作任务，旨在探索LLM在实际协作环境中的使用模式和影响。

Method: 采用探索性实验室研究，招募18对软件专业人员，让他们在自由使用LLM的情况下设计一个大学校园自行车停车应用。研究人员观察和分析参与者如何使用LLM进行协作设计。

Result: 研究发现：1）LLM引入导致不同的联合使用模式：共享实例使用促进共享理解，而跨单独实例的并行使用有时导致"上下文漂移"；2）专业人员对LLM的依赖程度差异很大，从不使用到将其视为信息源或生产者；3）在所有使用模式中，专业人员都会仔细审查和反思LLM的响应，这常常产生设计见解；4）早期锚定有时会限制探索。

Conclusion: 研究为设计辅助工具提供了启示，强调在保留设计过程中以人为本的重要性的同时，开发能够支持设计师的工具。LLM在协作设计中的使用需要平衡自动化支持与人类创造性和批判性思维。

Abstract: While much prior work examines Large Language Models (LLMs) for solo development tasks (e.g., coding), far less is known about how LLMs shape collaborative group work in software engineering. This study focuses on one such collaborative task, namely software design. It presents the results of an exploratory laboratory study of 18 pairs of software professionals who could use an LLM however they saw fit, to design a University campus bicycle parking application. Our findings reveal that introducing an LLM leads to distinct patterns of joint use: shared-instance use facilitated shared understanding, whereas parallel use across separate instances sometimes led to ''context drift''. We also observe wide variation in reliance, from non-use to treating the LLM as an information source or producer. Across these modes, professionals scrutinized and reflected on LLM responses, often yielding design insights; however, early anchoring sometimes curtailed exploration. We provide implications for tools to aid designers while retaining the human-centricity important to design.

</details>


### [138] [SHIFT: Sigmoid-Based Heuristic Invertible Fitness-Landscape Transformation for Accelerating SBST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09171)
*Jeongjin Han,Seunghoon Sim,Jian Lee,Seongyoon Park*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SHIFT方法通过Sigmoid压缩变换优化SBST的适应度地形，解决局部最优和高原停滞问题，提升测试覆盖收敛速度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的软件测试（SBST）常受限于具有欺骗性局部最优和缺乏信息的高原区域的挑战性适应度地形，这些地形阻碍搜索进展并降低测试生成效率

Method: 提出SHIFT（基于Sigmoid的可逆启发式适应度地形变换加速SBST），通过系统压缩密集区域来收缩局部地形，保持映射可逆性，使优化算法能以相同步长更有效地向全局覆盖移动

Result: 与纯爬山算法和遗传算法等基线方法相比，在标准化实验协议下，SHIFT在收敛速度和搜索效率方面取得一致改进

Conclusion: Sigmoid压缩构成了一种轻量级但有效的机制，能够在复杂测试环境中实现更可靠的覆盖发现

Abstract: Search-Based Software Testing (SBST) automates test input generation but is frequently hindered by challenging fitness landscapes characterized by numerous deceptive local optima that impede search progress, as well as extended plateaus where informative fitness signals are scarce. To address this bottleneck, we propose SHIFT (Sigmoid-Based Heuristic Invertible Fitness-Landscape Transformation for Accelerating SBST), a method designed to compress local landscapes and facilitate escape from stagnant regions without altering global semantics. By systematically contracting dense regions where search points cluster, the approach preserves mapping invertibility while enabling optimization algorithms to traverse more effectively toward global coverage with the same step size. When evaluated against established baselines, including pure hill climbing and genetic algorithms, under a normalized experimental protocol, the proposed technique yields consistent improvements in convergence speed and search efficiency. These results demonstrate that sigmoid compression constitutes a lightweight yet effective mechanism for achieving more reliable coverage discovery in complex testing environments.

</details>


### [139] [Toward an Architectural Blueprint to Observe Sustainability in and by Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09278)
*Klervie Toczé,Andrei Dragomir,Vincenzo Stoico,Patricia Lago*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个包含部署代码的架构蓝图，用于简化软件系统中可观测性的添加，特别关注软件能耗测量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统的可观测性带来诸多好处（如问题识别、改进实施），尤其对于可持续性相关维度的观测至关重要。然而，添加可观测性（特别是软件可持续性方面）需要技术知识，而并非所有受益项目都具备这些知识。

Method: 提出一个架构蓝图及其部署代码，用于促进软件系统中可观测性的添加。该工具包支持定义特定用例所需的组件并结构化其部署。特别地，它包含了软件能耗测量功能。通过两个不同用例展示了可观测性的添加过程。

Result: 开发了一个包含部署代码的架构蓝图工具包，能够简化可观测性（特别是能耗测量）的添加过程。通过两个用例验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的架构蓝图和部署代码工具包能够降低添加可观测性（特别是可持续性维度）的技术门槛，使更多项目能够受益于系统观测，从而更好地了解和缓解软件系统的影响。

Abstract: Enabling observability in software systems brings many benefits. It can, for example, ease the identification of issues or the implementation of improvements. It is especially critical to be able to observe sustainability-related dimensions of systems to know and mitigate their impact. However, adding observability to a system, especially related to software sustainability, requires technical knowledge that may not be available in every project that would benefit from it.
  In this work, we propose an architectural blueprint along with its deployment code that can be used to facilitate the addition of observability in software systems. As a special case, it includes measuring the energy consumption of software. This toolkit provides support in defining which components are necessary for a given use case and for structuring their deployment. Moreover, we exemplify the addition of observability in two different use cases.

</details>


### [140] [The Need for a Green ICT Reference Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09307)
*Marco Aiello,Mina Alipour,Antonio Brogi,Rafael Capilla,Lidia Fuentes,Ilche Georgievski,Gabriele Gianini,Mahyar Tourchi Moghaddam,Monica Vitali,Sebastian Werner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出需要统一的绿色ICT参考框架来解决ICT系统可持续性评估的复杂性、碎片化测量和职责不清等问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICT系统的可持续性影响难以评估和管理，原因包括结构复杂性、碎片化的测量实践以及系统各层职责不清。这些挑战无法仅通过指标来解决，需要建立共享的参考框架

Method: 在Informatics Europe绿色ICT工作组内开发初步框架，作为全面参考框架的第一步。该框架整合了多个视角和领域、生命周期阶段以及治理背景下的可持续性

Result: 提出了一个初始的绿色ICT参考框架，旨在为ICT系统的可持续性评估和管理提供统一的参考标准

Conclusion: 需要建立共享的绿色ICT参考框架来应对ICT系统可持续性评估的复杂挑战，该框架应整合多视角、多领域、全生命周期和治理背景，工作组提出的初步框架是迈向全面框架的重要第一步

Abstract: The sustainability impacts of ICT systems are difficult to assess and govern due to structural complexity, fragmented measurement practices, and unclear responsibilities across system layers. We argue that these challenges cannot be addressed solely by metrics and motivate the need for a shared Green ICT reference framework that integrates sustainability across multiple perspectives and domains, lifecycle phases, and governance contexts. We present an initial framework developed within the Informatics Europe Green ICT Working Group as a first step towards a comprehensive reference framework.

</details>


### [141] [LLM-Rosetta: A Hub-and-Spoke Intermediate Representation for Cross-Provider LLM API Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09360)
*Peng Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM-Rosetta是一个开源翻译框架，通过中心辐射型中间表示（IR）实现不同LLM API之间的双向转换，解决API碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）提供商激增，每个提供商都暴露专有的API格式，导致生态系统碎片化。应用程序与单个供应商紧密耦合，切换或桥接提供商需要O(N²)的双边适配器，阻碍了可移植性和多提供商架构。

Method: 基于观察发现主要LLM API共享共同的语义核心，提出LLM-Rosetta框架：1）采用中心辐射型中间表示（IR），包含9类型内容模型和10类型流事件模式；2）模块化Ops组合转换器架构，每个API标准可独立添加；3）支持请求和响应负载的双向转换，包括具有状态上下文管理的分块级流式传输。

Result: 实现了四个API标准的转换器（OpenAI Chat Completions、OpenAI Responses、Anthropic Messages、Google GenAI），覆盖大多数商业提供商。实证评估显示：无损往返保真度、正确的流式行为、低于100微秒的转换开销，与LiteLLM的单向方法竞争，同时提供双向性和提供商中立性。通过Open Responses合规套件测试，已在阿贡国家实验室生产环境中部署。

Conclusion: LLM-Rosetta通过捕获共享语义核心的中间表示，有效解决了LLM API碎片化问题，实现了提供商中立、高效的双向转换，促进了应用程序的可移植性和多提供商架构。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Large Language Model (LLM) providers--each exposing proprietary API formats--has created a fragmented ecosystem where applications become tightly coupled to individual vendors. Switching or bridging providers requires $O(N^2)$ bilateral adapters, impeding portability and multi-provider architectures. We observe that despite substantial syntactic divergence, the major LLM APIs share a common semantic core: the practical challenge is the combinatorial surface of syntactic variations, not deep semantic incompatibility. Based on this finding, we present LLM-Rosetta, an open-source translation framework built on a hub-and-spoke Intermediate Representation (IR) that captures the shared semantic core--messages, content parts, tool calls, reasoning traces, and generation controls--in a 9-type content model and 10-type stream event schema. A modular Ops-composition converter architecture enables each API standard to be added independently. LLM-Rosetta supports bidirectional conversion (provider-to-IR-to-provider) for both request and response payloads, including chunk-level streaming with stateful context management. We implement converters for four API standards (OpenAI Chat Completions, OpenAI Responses, Anthropic Messages, and Google GenAI), covering the vast majority of commercial providers. Empirical evaluation demonstrates lossless round-trip fidelity, correct streaming behavior, and sub-100 microsecond conversion overhead--competitive with LiteLLM's single-pass approach while providing bidirectionality and provider neutrality. LLM-Rosetta passes the Open Responses compliance suite and is deployed in production at Argonne National Laboratory. Code is available at https://github.com/Oaklight/llm-rosetta.

</details>


### [142] [The AI Codebase Maturity Model: From Assisted Coding to Self-Sustaining Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09388)
*Andy Anderson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AI代码库成熟度模型（ACMM）：一个5级框架，描述代码库如何从基础AI辅助编码演进到自维持系统，强调反馈循环拓扑和无法跳级的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI编码工具广泛采用，但大多数团队停留在"提示-审查"阶段，缺乏系统化演进框架。需要建立结构化模型来指导代码库从AI辅助开发向自维持系统的演进路径。

Method: 提出AI代码库成熟度模型（ACMM），受CMMI启发，包含5个级别，每个级别由反馈循环拓扑定义。通过4个月的经验报告验证模型，维护KubeStellar Console（CNCF Kubernetes仪表板），使用Claude Code（Opus）和GitHub Copilot从头构建。

Result: 系统运行63个CI/CD工作流，32个夜间测试套件，91%代码覆盖率，实现24小时全天候30分钟内bug修复。核心发现：AI驱动开发系统的智能不在于AI模型本身，而在于围绕它的指令、测试、指标和反馈循环基础设施。

Conclusion: 无法跳过任何级别，每个级别解锁下一级的关键是另一个反馈机制。测试用例数量、覆盖率阈值和测试执行可靠性是整个旅程中最重要的投资。AI开发系统的智能源于其基础设施而非模型本身。

Abstract: AI coding tools are widely adopted, but most teams plateau at prompt-and-review without a framework for systematic progression. This paper presents the AI Codebase Maturity Model (ACMM), a 5-level framework describing how codebases evolve from basic AI-assisted coding to self-sustaining systems. Inspired by CMMI, each level is defined by its feedback loop topology the specific mechanisms that must exist before the next level becomes possible. I validate the model through a 4-month experience report maintaining KubeStellar Console, a CNCF Kubernetes dashboard built from scratch with Claude Code (Opus) and GitHub Copilot. The system currently operates with 63 CI/CD workflows, 32 nightly test suites, 91% code coverage, and achieves bug-to-fix times under 30 minutes 24 hours a day. The central finding: the intelligence of an AI-driven development system resides not in the AI model itself, but in the infrastructure of instructions, tests, metrics, and feedback loops that surround it. You cannot skip levels, and at each level, the thing that unlocks the next one is another feedback mechanism. Testing the volume of test cases, the coverage thresholds, and the reliability of test execution proved to be the single most important investment in the entire journey.

</details>


### [143] [When LLMs Lag Behind: Knowledge Conflicts from Evolving APIs in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09515)
*Ahmed Nusayer Ashik,Shaowei Wang,Tse-Hsun Chen,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM在软件库API演化（废弃、修改、新增）下的代码生成问题，发现即使提供最新API文档，LLM仍难以优先外部上下文，平均仅42.55%生成代码可执行，推理策略可提升11%可执行率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件库快速演化导致LLM的静态参数知识过时，虽然检索增强生成（RAG）可提供最新API规范，但存在"上下文-记忆冲突"问题——外部指令与模型内部参数知识相矛盾。需要系统研究LLM在API演化下的代码生成能力。

Method: 构建包含270个真实更新的基准测试，涵盖8个Python库的API废弃、修改和新增三种演化类型。评估4个LLM家族的11个模型，分析结构化文档、模型规模和推理策略（如自我反思）对性能的影响。

Result: 无全面文档时，LLM难以优先外部上下文，平均仅42.55%生成代码可执行。结构化文档和更大模型规模有所改善，但可执行率仍低至66.36%。推理策略显著提升性能，可执行率提高11%。LLM即使提供API更新规范，仍会坚持过时模式。

Conclusion: LLM在API演化下存在显著的"上下文-记忆冲突"问题，现有方法无法完全解决可执行性问题。需要演化感知的基准测试和技术来应对软件库快速变化带来的挑战。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of software libraries creates a significant challenge for Large Language Models (LLMs), whose static parametric knowledge often becomes stale post-training. While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is commonly used to provide up-to-date API specifications, "context-memory conflict" arises when external instructions contradict a model's internal parametric knowledge. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of LLM code generation under API evolution (e.g., API deprecation, API modification, and API addition), by constructing a benchmark of 270 real-world updates from eight Python libraries. We evaluate four LLM families of 11 models. Our results show that without comprehensive documentation, LLMs struggle to prioritize external context, averaging only 42.55% of generated code examples are executable in the target environment. While structured documentation and larger model scales improve LLMs' ability to update adoption, they do not fully resolve executability issues with a low 66.36% executable rate. In addition, reasoning-based strategies (e.g., Self-Reflection) significantly boost LLMs' performance with 11% improvement on executable rate. Our findings highlight the persistence of outdated patterns from LLMs, even when API update specifications are provided, and emphasize the need for evolution-aware benchmarks and techniques.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Multimodal Large Language Model Enabled Robust Beamforming for HAP Downlink Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09017)
*Xiaoyu Xing,Peng Yang,Guoquan Tao,Dingyi Lu,Zehui Xiong,Xianbin Cao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于多模态大语言模型的波束赋形框架，通过视觉-语言LLM预测高空平台姿态变化，实现鲁棒的延迟感知波束转向，提升通信性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高空平台姿态微小变化会导致下行波束方向显著偏差，严重降低通信性能。传统方法难以应对平台抖动带来的动态变化，需要更智能的波束管理方案

Method: 设计视觉-语言LLM从多元飞行遥测数据学习预测短期HAP姿态；开发离线预测误差校准程序获得误差上界；基于姿态预测主动更新模拟波束赋形器；提出QoS驱动的波束赋形和接纳方法，包含轻量级可行性强制步骤

Result: VL-LLM能准确捕捉HAP姿态变化；相比基准方法，用户服务率提高22.1%，总速率提高12.5%；平均总延迟36.24ms，p99延迟40.13ms，支持实际延迟感知部署

Conclusion: 提出的多模态LLM波束赋形框架能有效应对HAP姿态变化，实现鲁棒的下行通信，显著提升服务质量和系统性能，满足实际部署的延迟要求

Abstract: Small changes in high altitude platform (HAP) attitude can cause significant deviations in HAP downlink beam directions, thereby severely degrading HAP downlink communication performance. In this paper, we develop a multimodal large language model (LLM) enabled beamforming framework to achieve robust HAP downlink communications.Specifically, we design a vision-language LLM (VL-LLM) that learns from multivariate flight telemetry to forecast short-term HAP attitudes under platform shaking and support delay-aware proactive beam steering.We design an offline forecast-error calibration procedure to obtain upper bounds on forecast errors and improve the reliability of proactive analog beam steering.Based on the attitude forecasts, we proactively update the analog beamformer and propose a QoS-driven beamforming and admission method with a lightweight feasibility-enforcement step to satisfy instantaneous transmit-power and QoS requirements.Simulation results indicate that the designed VL-LLM can accurately capture changes in the HAP attitude and the proposed beamforming method achieves a 22.1% higher user service ratio and a 12.5% higher sum-rate than representative baselines.The measured mean and p99 total latencies are 36.24 ms and 40.13 ms, respectively, supporting practical delay-aware deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [qPRO-AQFP: Post-Routing Optimization of AQFP Circuits with Delay Line Clocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08705)
*Robert S. Aviles,Ziyu Liu,Jingkai Hong,Sasan Razmkhah,Massoud Pedram,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出频率感知的后布线优化框架，解决AQFP逻辑中传统固定时序参数无法捕获频率相关约束的问题，实现100%时序收敛和相位跳过自动化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AQFP逻辑作为超低功耗超导逻辑家族，在量子计算控制和低温计算系统中具有吸引力。传统AQFP设计面临严格的时钟要求和有限的互连长度，导致显著的缓冲开销和时序收敛困难。现有延迟线时钟方法主要关注布局和延迟最小化，但使用固定时序参数无法捕获AQFP建立和保持约束的频率依赖性。

Method: 提出频率感知的后布线优化框架，在用户指定权重下联合优化时钟周期、延迟和时序裕量。该方法自动化相位跳过，减少路径平衡缓冲插入，同时考虑AQFP时序约束的频率依赖性。

Result: 在常见基准测试中实现了100%的后布线时序收敛，覆盖了性能-延迟-裕量的权衡范围。相位跳过自动化使路径平衡缓冲插入平均减少34%，而工作频率仅降低4%。

Conclusion: 提出的频率感知优化框架有效解决了AQFP逻辑中的时序收敛问题，通过联合优化时钟周期、延迟和时序裕量，实现了全面的时序闭合，同时显著减少了缓冲开销。

Abstract: Adiabatic Quantum-Flux-Parametron (AQFP) logic is an ultra-low-power superconducting logic family with energy consumption approaching the Shannon limit, making it attractive for quantum computing control and cryogenic computing systems. Traditional AQFP designs face significant physical design challenges due to strict gate-level clocking requirements and limited interconnect lengths, leading to substantial buffer overhead and difficult timing closure. Recently, delay-line clocking of AQFP has been proposed to improve timing margins and reduce latency by enabling more flexible clock scheduling. However, prior work has primarily focused on placement and latency minimization, while relying on fixed timing parameters that do not capture the frequency dependence of AQFP setup and hold constraints. To address this limitation, we propose a frequency-aware post-routing optimization framework that jointly optimizes clock period, latency, and timing slack under user-specified weighting. Experimental results across common benchmarks achieve 100% post-routing timing closure across a range of performance--latency--slack trade-offs. Our approach also automates phase-skipping, reducing path-balancing buffer insertion by 34% on average while only reducing operating frequency by 4%.

</details>


### [146] [A Physically-Informed Subgraph Isomorphism Approach to Molecular Docking Using Quantum Annealers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09540)
*Francesco Micucci,Matteo Barbieri,Gabriella Bettonte,Domenico Bonanni,Anita Camillini,Anna Fava,Daniele Gregori,Andrea R. Beccari,Gianluca Palermo*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文在先前纯几何分子对接研究基础上，引入蛋白质-配体间的物理化学相互作用（库仑力、范德华力、氢键和疏水作用），构建了包含这些相互作用的QUBO公式，并使用D-Wave量子退火器验证其对对接结果准确性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究仅考虑几何形状的分子对接忽略了关键的物理化学相互作用，这限制了对接结果的准确性。为了改进分子对接的准确性，需要将蛋白质-配体间的物理化学相互作用纳入量子计算框架。

Method: 1. 在先前纯几何QUBO公式基础上，引入库仑力、范德华力、氢键和疏水作用作为修正项；2. 构建包含这些物理化学相互作用的新QUBO公式；3. 使用D-Wave量子退火器进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，引入物理化学相互作用的QUBO公式相比纯几何方法，显著提高了分子对接结果的准确性。

Conclusion: 将物理化学相互作用整合到量子退火框架中，能够有效提升分子对接的准确性，为基于量子计算的药物发现提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Molecular docking is a crucial step in the development of new drugs as it guides the positioning of a small molecule (ligand) within the pocket of a target protein. In the literature, a feasibility study explored the potential of D-Wave quantum annealers for purely geometric molecular docking, neglecting physicochemical interactions between the protein and the ligand and focusing solely on their simplified geometries. To achieve this, the ligands were represented as graphs incorporating their geometric properties and then mapped onto a grid that discretized the three-dimensional space of the protein pocket. The quality of the ligand pose on the protein pocket was evaluated through the isomorphism between the ligand graph and the spatial grid. This paper builds on the previous study by introducing physicochemical interactions between the protein-ligand pair into the QUBO problem to improve the accuracy of the docking results. This paper presents a novel QUBO formulation that includes Coulomb and van der Waals forces, together with components representing H-bond and hydrophobic interactions. We integrate these physical interactions as corrective terms to the previous purely geometric QUBO formulation, and provide experimental results using the D-Wave quantum annealers to demonstrate their impact on the accuracy of the docking results.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Choose, Don't Label: Multiple-Choice Query Synthesis for Program Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08792)
*Celeste Barnaby,Danny Ding,Osbert Bastani,Isil Dillig*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出基于选择题查询的程序消歧主动学习新范式，通过展示高级行为选项让用户选择，开发Socrates工具自动生成信息丰富的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码高级规范存在固有歧义，现有交互方法依赖标记示例监督，但示例容易出错且难以准确捕捉用户意图

Method: 引入基于选择题查询的主动学习范式：系统展示少量高级行为作为选择题选项，用户选择意图选项；每个选项对应描述语义相似候选程序簇的Hoare三元组；开发Socrates工具自动合成信息丰富的选择题查询

Result: 在四个领域（符号和神经符号设置）评估Socrates：产生直观易答的查询，实现高效收敛；比现有方法更可靠地识别目标程序，同时保持有竞争力的运行时性能

Conclusion: 基于选择题查询的主动学习范式为程序消歧提供了更可靠、直观的方法，Socrates工具在多种设置下表现出色

Abstract: High-level specifications of code are inherently ambiguous, and prior systems have explored interactive techniques to help users clarify their intent and resolve such ambiguities. However, most existing approaches elicit supervision through labeled examples, which are often error-prone and may fail to capture user intent. This paper introduces a new active learning paradigm for program disambiguation based on multiple-choice queries. In this paradigm, the system presents a small set of high-level behaviors as multiple-choice options, and the user simply selects the intended one. Technically, each answer option corresponds to a Hoare triple that characterizes a cluster of semantically similar candidate programs. This formulation enables formal reasoning about the informativeness and interpretability of queries, and supports systematic construction of optimal queries. Building on this insight, we develop a new active learning algorithm and implement it in a tool called Socrates, which automatically synthesizes informative multiple-choice queries for program disambiguation. We evaluate Socrates across four domains spanning both symbolic and neurosymbolic settings and show that it produces intuitive, easy-to-answer queries and achieves efficient convergence. Most importantly, Socrates identifies the intended program more reliably than existing methods, while maintaining competitive runtime performance.

</details>


### [148] [A Deductive System for Contract Satisfaction Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09165)
*Arthur Correnson,Haoyi Zeng,Jana Hofmann*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在交互式证明助手中验证硬件-软件契约满足性的方法，通过相对迹相等和相对互模拟概念，开发了可证明完备的演绎证明系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件-软件契约是CPU泄漏行为的抽象规范，可用于验证高级程序对抗侧信道攻击的安全性。当前证明契约满足性主要依赖模型检查方法，本文探索如何在交互式证明助手中进行此类证明。

Method: 将契约满足性视为相对迹相等问题的实例，引入相对互模拟作为证明技术。基于共归纳证明的最新进展，开发了相对迹相等的演绎证明系统，该系统具有可证明的完备性，支持模块化和增量式证明风格，并利用对称性和传递性简化证明。

Result: 在Rocq证明助手中形式化了演绎证明系统，并将其应用于两个具有挑战性的契约满足性证明，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文展示了在交互式证明助手中进行硬件-软件契约满足性证明的可行性，提出的相对互模拟方法和演绎证明系统为形式化验证侧信道安全提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: Hardware-software contracts are abstract specifications of a CPU's leakage behavior. They enable verifying the security of high-level programs against side-channel attacks without having to explicitly reason about the microarchitectural details of the CPU. Using the abstraction powers of a contract requires proving that the targeted CPU satisfies the contract in the sense that the contract over-approximates the CPU's leakage. Besides pen-and-paper reasoning, proving contract satisfaction has been approached mostly from the model-checking perspective, with approaches based on a (semi-)automated search for the necessary invariants.
  As an alternative, this paper explores how such proofs can be conducted in interactive proof assistants. We start by observing that contract satisfaction is an instance of a more general problem we call relative trace equality, and we introduce relative bisimulation as an associated proof technique. Leveraging recent advances in the field of coinductive proofs, we develop a deductive proof system for relative trace equality. Our system is provably sound and complete, and it enables a modular and incremental proof style. It also features several reasoning principles to simplify proofs by exploiting symmetries and transitivity properties. We formalized our deductive system in the Rocq proof assistant and applied it to two challenging contract satisfaction proofs.

</details>


### [149] [Tracers for debugging and program exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09301)
*Shardul Chiplunkar,Clément Pit-Claudel*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出基于执行轨迹的调试工具新方法，强调按时间顺序而非语法顺序展示代码执行


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调试工具更适合测试假设而非生成假设，步进调试器只提供程序状态的孤立快照，需要程序员在脑中重构状态随时间的变化

Method: 围绕程序执行的完整历史（轨迹）构建调试和程序探索工具，让用户按时间顺序而非语法顺序查看每一行代码的执行

Result: 讨论了设计选择、初步结果和有趣挑战

Conclusion: 基于执行轨迹的调试方法能更好地支持程序理解和调试过程，特别是假设生成阶段

Abstract: Programmers often use an iterative process of hypothesis generation ("perhaps this function is called twice?") and hypothesis testing ("let's count how many times this breakpoint fires") to understand the behavior of unfamiliar or malfunctioning software. Existing debugging tools are much better suited to testing hypotheses than to generating them. Step debuggers, for example, present isolated snapshots of the program's state, leaving it to the programmer to mentally reconstruct the evolution of that state over time. We advocate for a different approach: building a debugging and program-exploration tool around a *trace*, or complete history, of the program's execution. Our key claim is that the user should see every line *as executed* (in time order) rather than *as written* (in syntax order). We discuss design choices, preliminary results, and interesting challenges.

</details>


### [150] [CIR+CVN: Bridging LLM Semantic Understanding and Petri-Net Verification for Concurrent Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09318)
*Kaiwen Zhang,Guanjun Liu*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于规范驱动、模型优先的验证架构，使用LLM从自然语言需求生成并发中间表示(Cir)，然后转换为并发验证网(Cvn)进行形式化验证，支持迭代的错误检测和修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从源代码恢复并发结构很困难，因为共享资源标识和保护关系常被别名、所有权和API特定习惯用法所掩盖。需要一种更可靠的方法来构建和验证并发程序。

Method: 采用规范驱动、模型优先的验证架构：1) LLM从自然语言需求生成Cir（并发中间表示），这是语句级、无别名的模型；2) 将验证后的Cir转换为Cvn（并发验证网），这是带权Petri网；3) 进行穷举探索，将反例映射回语句标识以指导修复；4) 使用轻量级目标可达性检查过滤行为丢失的修复。

Result: 在9个代表性有界并发模式上评估，结果显示该方法支持对Cir工件的迭代错误检测和修复，目标可达性有助于过滤语义不完整的修复。信任边界是生成的Cir工件而非任意源代码。

Conclusion: 该方法通过形式化表示和机械转换，为LLM辅助的并发程序构建提供了可靠的验证架构，将信任边界从任意源代码转移到可验证的中间表示，提高了并发程序构建的可靠性。

Abstract: Recovering concurrency structure directly from source code is difficult because shared-resource identity and protection relations are often obscured by aliasing, ownership, and API-specific idioms. We therefore study a specification-driven, model-first verification architecture for LLM-assisted concurrent program construction. Instead of verifying arbitrary source code, a large language model first synthesizes a verification-oriented concurrency artifact from a natural-language requirement or system specification. The first formalism, the Concurrency Intermediate Representation (Cir), is a statement-level, alias-free model in which shared resources are globally named, protection relations are explicit, and each statement carries a stable identifier. The second formalism, the Concurrency Verification Net (Cvn), is a weighted place/transition Petri net with a finite global store and three-valued guards for data-dependent branching. A validated Cir artifact is translated mechanically to Cvn, explored exhaustively, and any counterexample is mapped back to statement identifiers to guide targeted repair. To reduce the risk of bug-free but behavior-dropping repairs, acceptance additionally applies a lightweight goal-reachability check over designated critical outcomes. We formalize both representations, prove translation-correspondence results for deadlock and signal-loss analysis, define a two-layer checking architecture with 61 static rules and 5 analysis predicates, and evaluate the pipeline on 9 representative bounded-concurrency patterns. The results show that the method supports iterative bug detection and repair on Cir artifacts and that goal reachability helps filter semantically incomplete repairs. The trust boundary of the present work is the generated Cir artifact rather than arbitrary source code.

</details>
